" ============================================================================
" XSA vs SEGW functional JSON parity test - paged low-memory implementation
"
" Memory strategy:
" - XSA fixture is indexed once with PO/item and object character offsets only.
" - Raw SEGW JSON is fetched one SKIP/TOP page at a time.
" - JSON properties are read directly from both raw payloads for each matched row.
" - Comparison is strict except for documented, field-specific SEGW mappings of
"   XSA SQL NULL values and a temporary unordered-aggregate compatibility rule.
" - No complete XSA row table, no complete property tree, and no copied JSON
"   objects are held in memory.
"
" Paging relies on the stable ORDER BY implemented by all dashboard flows.
" ============================================================================

CLASS ltc_parity DEFINITION DEFERRED.
CLASS zcl_fi_das_dashboard DEFINITION LOCAL FRIENDS ltc_parity.

CLASS lcx_parity_error DEFINITION INHERITING FROM zcx_fi_das_error.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    METHODS if_message~get_text REDEFINITION.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcx_parity_error IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD if_message~get_text.
    result = reason.
    IF result IS INITIAL.
      result = 'Parity helper failed without a supplied reason'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcx_silent_error DEFINITION INHERITING FROM zcx_fi_das_error.
  PUBLIC SECTION.
    METHODS if_message~get_text REDEFINITION.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS lcx_silent_error IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD if_message~get_text.
    CLEAR result.
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.

CLASS ltc_parity DEFINITION
  FINAL
  FOR TESTING
  RISK LEVEL HARMLESS
  DURATION LONG.

  PRIVATE SECTION.

* GROUP TESTS - aggregate results; run the class for native leaf results

    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_case,
        fixture       TYPE string,
        email         TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_email,
        poowner       TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_poowner,
        flow          TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_mode,
        pstype        TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>t_pstype,
        filter_select_options TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_filter_select_options,
        filter_raw    TYPE string,
        orderby       TYPE string,
        orderby_desc  TYPE abap_bool,
        select_list   TYPE string,
        top           TYPE i,
        top_specified TYPE abap_bool,
        inlinecount   TYPE abap_bool,
      END OF ts_case,

      BEGIN OF ts_expected_index,
        ponumber    TYPE string,
        itemno      TYPE string,
        object_from TYPE i,
        object_to   TYPE i,
        matched     TYPE abap_bool,
      END OF ts_expected_index,

      tt_expected_index TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ts_expected_index
        WITH UNIQUE KEY ponumber itemno,

      BEGIN OF ts_property_name,
        name TYPE string,
      END OF ts_property_name,

      tt_property_name TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ts_property_name
        WITH UNIQUE KEY name,

      tt_string TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF string
        WITH EMPTY KEY,

      BEGIN OF ts_json_property,
        name  TYPE string,
        token TYPE string,
      END OF ts_json_property,

      tt_json_property TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ts_json_property
        WITH UNIQUE KEY name.

    CONSTANTS:
      mc_mime_root TYPE string VALUE '/SAP/PUBLIC/DAS/',
      mc_max_log   TYPE i VALUE 20,
      mc_max_token TYPE i VALUE 256,
      mc_utf8_bom TYPE x LENGTH 3 VALUE 'EFBBBF',

*     Single paging limit for Over, Under and Exclude.
*     Reduce this value when the productive system has a smaller heap.
      mc_page_size TYPE i VALUE 1000.

    DATA:
      mt_differences       TYPE stringtab,
      mv_difference_count  TYPE i,
      mv_assert_quit TYPE aunit_flowctrl VALUE if_aunit_constants=>method,

      mv_phase TYPE string,
      mv_parity_reported TYPE abap_bool,
      mr_failure_log TYPE REF TO stringtab,
      mv_unused TYPE abap_bool.

    DATA mo_cut TYPE REF TO zcl_fi_das_dashboard.

        zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS compare_raw_object
      IMPORTING
        i_fixture          TYPE string
        i_ponumber         TYPE string
        i_itemno           TYPE string
        i_expected_json    TYPE string
        i_expected_from    TYPE i
        i_expected_to      TYPE i
        i_actual_json      TYPE string
        i_actual_from      TYPE i
        i_actual_to        TYPE i
      RAISING
        zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS read_object_properties
      IMPORTING
        i_json              TYPE string
        i_object_from       TYPE i
        i_object_to         TYPE i
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rt_properties) TYPE tt_json_property
      RAISING
        zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS compare_raw_property
      IMPORTING
        i_fixture       TYPE string
        i_ponumber      TYPE string
        i_itemno        TYPE string
        i_name          TYPE string
        i_expected_token TYPE string
        i_actual_token   TYPE string
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS is_equivalent_null
      IMPORTING
        i_name           TYPE string
        i_expected_token TYPE string
        i_actual_token   TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_result) TYPE abap_bool.

    METHODS normalize_odata_time
      IMPORTING
        i_token         TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_token) TYPE string
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS ignore_missing_property
      IMPORTING
        i_name           TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_result) TYPE abap_bool.

    METHODS is_unordered_aggregate_field
      IMPORTING
        i_name           TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_result) TYPE abap_bool.

    METHODS equal_aggregate_content
      IMPORTING
        i_expected      TYPE string
        i_actual        TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_equal) TYPE abap_bool.

    METHODS split_aggregate_empty
      IMPORTING
        i_value          TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rt_tokens) TYPE tt_string.

    METHODS read_json_string_end
      IMPORTING
                i_text        TYPE string
                i_start       TYPE i
      RETURNING
                VALUE(rv_end) TYPE i
      RAISING   zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS read_json_value_end
      IMPORTING
                i_text        TYPE string
                i_start       TYPE i
      RETURNING
                VALUE(rv_end) TYPE i
      RAISING   zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS find_matching_end
      IMPORTING
                i_text        TYPE string
                i_start       TYPE i
                i_open        TYPE char1
                i_close       TYPE char1
      RETURNING
                VALUE(rv_end) TYPE i
      RAISING   zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS skip_whitespace
      IMPORTING
        i_text             TYPE string
        i_position         TYPE i
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_position) TYPE i
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS is_whitespace
      IMPORTING
        i_char           TYPE char1
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_result) TYPE abap_bool.

    METHODS trim_text
      IMPORTING
        i_text         TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_text) TYPE string.

    METHODS unquote_json
      IMPORTING
        i_token         TYPE string
      RETURNING
        VALUE(rv_value) TYPE string
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS add_difference
      IMPORTING
        i_text TYPE string.

    METHODS assert_no_differences
      IMPORTING i_title TYPE string DEFAULT 'DAS XSA vs SEGW OData parity failed.'.

    METHODS extract_range
      IMPORTING it_filters TYPE zif_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_filter_select_options
      RETURNING VALUE(rs_request) TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS filter_rows
      IMPORTING i_predicate TYPE string
      CHANGING ct_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS check_select
      IMPORTING i_select TYPE string i_orderby TYPE string OPTIONAL
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.

    METHODS matches_complex_fixture
      IMPORTING it_properties TYPE tt_json_property
      RETURNING VALUE(rv_match) TYPE abap_bool
      RAISING zcx_fi_das_error.
ENDCLASS.

  METHOD single_star_wildcard.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lt_wildcard) = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = `*` ).
    DATA(lt_mixed) = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = `*, ab01` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (single_star_wildcard)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_wildcard[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (single_star_wildcard)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_mixed[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (single_star_wildcard)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_mixed[ value = `*` ] ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD scope_field_normalization.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lt_company) = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = `ab01` ).
    DATA(lt_cost_center) = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = `123` ).
    DATA(lt_management_unit) = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = `Unit A` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ value = `AB01` ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[ value = `0000000123` ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[ value = `Unit A` ] ) ) ).

    lt_company = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = ` , , ` ).
    lt_cost_center = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = ` , , ` ).
    lt_management_unit = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = ` , , ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lt_company ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lt_cost_center ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lt_management_unit ).

    lt_company = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = ` ab01 , AB01, ab01 , ` ).
    lt_cost_center = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = ` 123,0000000123,123, ` ).
    lt_management_unit = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = ` Unit A,Unit   A,unit a, ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_company )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ value = 'AB01' wildcard = abap_false ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_cost_center )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[ value = '0000000123' ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_management_unit )
      exp  = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[ value = 'Unit A' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_management_unit[ value = 'unit a' ] ) ) ).

    lt_company = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = `NULL` ).
    lt_cost_center = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = `NULL,NULL` ).
    lt_management_unit = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = `NULL,NULL` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_company )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ value = 'NULL' wildcard = abap_false ] ) )
      msg  = 'Company NULL is a literal code, not a nullable-field marker' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_cost_center )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[
        value = '' wildcard = abap_false is_null = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_management_unit )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[
        value = '' wildcard = abap_false is_null = abap_true ] ) ) ).

    lt_company = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = ` *,ab01,AB01,NULL,, ` ).
    lt_cost_center = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = ` *,123,NULL,123,, ` ).
    lt_management_unit = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = ` *,Unit A,NULL,Unit A,, ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_company )
      exp  = 3 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_cost_center )
      exp  = 3 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_management_unit )
      exp  = 3 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ value = '*' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_company[ value = 'AB01' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_company[ value = 'NULL' ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[ value = '*' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_cost_center[ value = '0000000123' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_cost_center[ is_null = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[ value = '*' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_management_unit[ value = 'Unit A' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_management_unit[ is_null = abap_true ] ) ) ).

    lt_company = mo_cut->get_company_codes( i_value = ` , * , ` ).
    lt_cost_center = mo_cut->get_cost_centers( i_value = ` , * , ` ).
    lt_management_unit = mo_cut->get_management_units( i_value = ` , * , ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_company )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_cost_center )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = lines( lt_management_unit )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_company[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_cost_center[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(

      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (scope_field_normalization)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_management_unit[ wildcard = abap_true ] ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

* INDIVIDUAL TESTS - SORT PAGING
************************************************************************
  METHOD test_inlinecount_with_top_one.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case-top = 1.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 1
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 )
      exp  = 1
      msg  = '$top=1 must return exactly one row on the first page' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count_1 > 1 )
      msg  = '__count must represent the total filtered set, not the page size' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count_2
      exp  = lv_count_1
      msg  = '__count must stay stable across pages' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp  = 1
      msg  = 'The second top=1 page must still contain one row when total count > 1' ).

    assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
      i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
      i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

    assert_row_keys_match(
      i_json_left      = lv_json_page_1
      i_left_index     = 1
      i_json_right     = lv_json_page_2
      i_right_index    = 1
      i_expected_match = abap_false ).
    assert_page_windows(
      is_case = ls_case i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1 i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_no_inlinecount.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_no_inlinecount)|
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json )
      exp  = 1 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_skip_beyond_result_set.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case-top = 1.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 999999
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp  = 0
      msg  = '$skip beyond the result set must return an empty results array' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count_2
      exp  = lv_count_1
      msg  = '__count must remain valid even when the page is empty' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_top_zero.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case_full TYPE ts_case,
      ls_case_zero TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_full TYPE string,
      lv_json_zero TYPE string,
      lv_count_full TYPE int8,
      lv_count_zero TYPE int8.

    ls_case_full-email =
      'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.

    ls_case_full-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case_full-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    ls_case_full-top = 1.
    ls_case_full-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case_full-inlinecount = abap_true.

    ls_case_zero = ls_case_full.
    ls_case_zero-top = 0.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_full
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_full
        e_inline_count = lv_count_full ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = get_result_count( lv_json_full ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = xsdbool( lv_count_full > 0 )
      msg = 'A positive baseline is required to distinguish explicit zero top' ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_zero
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_zero
        e_inline_count = lv_count_zero ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_zero )
      exp  = 0
      msg  = '$top=0 must not return any result rows' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count_zero
      exp  = lv_count_full
      msg  = '$top=0 must preserve the same total inline count' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_sort_and_paging.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com' flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
      filter_raw = `POCURRENCY eq 'JPY'` orderby = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      top = 100 top_specified = abap_true inlinecount = abap_true ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_page_1) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count_1) ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
                                    i_skip = 100
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_page_2) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count_2) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_sort_and_paging)|
      act  = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_page_1 ) > 0 ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_sort_and_paging)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count_1 >= get_result_count( lv_page_1 ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_sort_and_paging)|
      act  = lv_count_2
      exp  = lv_count_1 ).
    assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_page_1
                                  i_property = 'POCURRENCY'
                                  i_value    = 'JPY' ).
    assert_rows_sorted( i_json     = lv_page_1
                        i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ).
    IF lv_count_1 > 100.
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_sort_and_paging)|
        act  = get_result_count( lv_page_2 ) ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_sort_and_paging)|
        act  = xsdbool( read_result_property( i_json = lv_page_1
          i_index = get_result_count( lv_page_1 ) i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' )
          <= read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_page_2 i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ) ) ).
    ENDIF.
    assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_page_2
                                  i_property = 'POCURRENCY'
                                  i_value    = 'JPY' ).
    assert_rows_sorted( i_json     = lv_page_2
                        i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ).
    assert_no_duplica_across_pages( i_json_page_1 = lv_page_1
                                    i_json_page_2 = lv_page_2 ).
    assert_page_windows(
      is_case = ls_case i_json_page_1 = lv_page_1 i_json_page_2 = lv_page_2 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_under_paging.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_page_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_page_2 TYPE string,
      lv_count_1     TYPE int8,
      lv_count_2     TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_1
        e_inline_count = lv_count_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_page_2
        e_inline_count = lv_count_2 ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_page_1 )
      exp  = 100
      msg  = 'The first Under page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_page_2 )
      exp  = 100
      msg  = 'The second Under page must honor $top=100' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count_2
      exp  = lv_count_1
      msg  = 'Under inline count must stay stable across pages' ).

    assert_no_duplica_across_pages(
      i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1
      i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

    assert_row_keys_match(
      i_json_left      = lv_json_page_1
      i_left_index     = 100
      i_json_right     = lv_json_page_2
      i_right_index    = 1
      i_expected_match = abap_false ).
    assert_page_windows(
      is_case = ls_case i_json_page_1 = lv_json_page_1 i_json_page_2 = lv_json_page_2 ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_excl_pstype_pages.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case        TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_desc_1 TYPE string,
      lv_json_desc_2 TYPE string,
      lv_json_asc_1  TYPE string,
      lv_json_asc_2  TYPE string,
      lv_count       TYPE i.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all.
    ls_case-orderby = 'PSTYPE'.
    ls_case-orderby_desc = abap_true.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_desc_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_desc_2 ).

    assert_page_windows(
      is_case = ls_case i_json_page_1 = lv_json_desc_1 i_json_page_2 = lv_json_desc_2 ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_desc_1
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_true ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_desc_2
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_true ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool(
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_desc_1
                  i_index    = get_result_count( lv_json_desc_1 )
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) )
        >=
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_desc_2
                  i_index    = 1
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) ) )
      msg  = 'Descending PSTYPE order must stay correct across the page boundary' ).

    CLEAR ls_case-orderby_desc.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 0
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_asc_1 ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 100
      IMPORTING
        e_json  = lv_json_asc_2 ).

    assert_page_windows(
      is_case = ls_case i_json_page_1 = lv_json_asc_1 i_json_page_2 = lv_json_asc_2 ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_asc_1
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_false ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json_asc_2
      i_property   = 'PSTYPE'
      i_descending = abap_false ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool(
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_asc_1
                  i_index    = get_result_count( lv_json_asc_1 )
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) )
        <=
        CONV i( read_result_property(
                  i_json     = lv_json_asc_2
                  i_index    = 1
                  i_property = 'PSTYPE' ) ) )
      msg  = 'Ascending PSTYPE order must stay correct across the page boundary' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_orderby_invalid.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA ls_case TYPE ts_case.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-orderby = 'NOT_A_COLUMN'.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.

    DATA(lv_path) =
      build_resource_path(
        is_case = ls_case
        i_skip  = 0 ).

    execute_gateway_raw(
      EXPORTING
        i_resource_path = lv_path
      IMPORTING
        e_status_code   = DATA(lv_status_code)
        e_status_text   = DATA(lv_status_text)
        e_json          = DATA(lv_json) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_status_code
      exp  = 400
      msg  = 'Unknown $orderby must be rejected with HTTP 400' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( lv_json CS '"error"' )
      msg  = 'Unknown $orderby must return a well-formed OData error body' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( to_upper( lv_json ) CS 'SHORT DUMP' )
      msg  = 'Unknown $orderby must not serialize an ABAP short dump' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( lv_status_text IS NOT INITIAL )
      msg  = 'The raw Gateway helper must expose the HTTP status text' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_sort_reviewed_by.

    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-orderby = 'REVIWED_BY'.
    ls_case-orderby_desc = abap_true.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_sort_reviewed_by)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count >= get_result_count( lv_json ) ) ).

    assert_rows_sorted(
      i_json       = lv_json
      i_property   = 'REVIWED_BY'
      i_descending = abap_true ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_skip_high_sorted.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json  TYPE string,
      lv_count TYPE int8.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-orderby = 'SUPPLIERNAME'.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
        i_skip         = 999999
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json )
      exp  = 0
      msg  = 'A high skip on a sorted request must return an empty result page' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count > 0 )
      msg  = 'The inline count must still expose the real filtered result size' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD sort_adds_default_ties.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lt_input) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000004' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000005' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' suppliername = 'Other' ) ).
    DATA(lt_expected) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000000005' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' suppliername = 'Other' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000004' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' suppliername = 'Same' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pstype = '0' suppliername = 'Same' ) ).
    DATA(lt_result) = lt_input.
    mo_cut->apply_sort_and_paging(
      EXPORTING is_request = VALUE #( orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) ) )
      CHANGING ct_result   = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (sort_adds_default_ties)|
      act  = lt_result exp = lt_expected ).
    DATA lt_joined TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result.
    DO 3 TIMES.
      DATA(lv_skip) = ( sy-index - 1 ) * 2.
      lt_result = lt_input.
      mo_cut->apply_sort_and_paging(
        EXPORTING is_request = VALUE #(
          orderby = VALUE #( ( property = `SUPPLIERNAME` ) )
          paging = VALUE #( skip = lv_skip top = 2 top_requested = abap_true ) )
        CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
      APPEND LINES OF lt_result TO lt_joined.
    ENDDO.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_joined exp = lt_expected
      msg = 'Concatenated pages must retain complete membership and every tie level' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD default_exclude_key_order.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lv_fixture) = read_mime_fixture( 'xsa-exclude.json' ).
    DATA(lt_index) = build_expected_index( lv_fixture ).
    DATA lt_order TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_key WITH EMPTY KEY.
    LOOP AT lt_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_index>).
      DATA(lt_properties) = read_object_properties(
        i_json        = lv_fixture
        i_object_from = <ls_index>-object_from
        i_object_to   = <ls_index>-object_to ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_properties[ name = 'PSTYPE' ] ) ) ).
      DATA(lv_type_token) = lt_properties[ name = 'PSTYPE' ]-token.
      APPEND VALUE #( ponumber = <ls_index>-ponumber itemno = <ls_index>-itemno
        pstype = unquote_json( lv_type_token ) ) TO lt_order.
    ENDLOOP.
    FREE: lv_fixture, lt_index, lt_properties.
    SORT lt_order BY pstype DESCENDING ponumber itemno.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_order[ pstype = '9' ] )
                AND line_exists( lt_order[ pstype = '0' ] ) )
      msg  = 'The real exclusion fixture must exercise both PSTYPE groups' ).
    READ TABLE lt_order TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY pstype = '0'.
    DATA(lv_boundary) = sy-tabix.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_boundary > 1 ) ).
    DATA lt_positions TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF i WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_positions = VALUE #( ( 1 ) ( lv_boundary - 1 ) ( lv_boundary ) ( lines( lt_order ) ) ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com' flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all
      top = 1 top_specified = abap_true inlinecount = abap_true ).
    LOOP AT lt_positions INTO DATA(lv_position).
      get_gateway_response(
        EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
                  i_skip = lv_position - 1
        IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_json) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count) ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = lv_count
        exp  = lines( lt_order ) ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = get_result_count( lv_json )
        exp  = 1 ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_json i_property = 'PSTYPE' )
        exp  = lt_order[ lv_position ]-pstype ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_json i_property = 'PONUMBER' )
        exp  = lt_order[ lv_position ]-ponumber ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (default_exclude_key_order)|
        act  = read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_json i_property = 'ITEMNO' )
        exp  = lt_order[ lv_position ]-itemno ).
      FREE lv_json.
    ENDLOOP.
    assert_no_differences( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_all_pstype_request.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com' flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all
      select_list = 'PONUMBER,ITEMNO,PSTYPE'
      top = 1000 top_specified = abap_true inlinecount = abap_true ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_json) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count) ).
    DATA(lv_rows) = get_result_count( lv_json ).
    assert_fixture_window( is_case = ls_case i_json = lv_json i_count = lv_count ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_all_pstype_request)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count >= lv_rows ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_all_pstype_request)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_rows > 0 ) ).
    DO lv_rows TIMES.
      DATA(lv_pstype) = read_result_property(
        i_json     = lv_json
        i_index    = sy-index
        i_property = 'PSTYPE' ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_all_pstype_request)|
        act  = xsdbool( lv_pstype = '0' OR lv_pstype = '9' ) ).
    ENDDO.
    " Validate each single-type result against the corresponding combined view.
    DATA(lt_types) = VALUE tt_string( ( `0` ) ( `9` ) ).
    DATA lv_combined_count TYPE int8.
    LOOP AT lt_types INTO DATA(lv_type).
      ls_case-pstype = lv_type.
      get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
        IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_single) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_single_count) ).
      DATA(ls_single_case) = ls_case.
      assert_fixture_window(
        is_case = ls_single_case i_json = lv_single i_count = lv_single_count ).
      assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_single
                                    i_property = 'PSTYPE'
                                    i_value    = lv_type ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_all_pstype_request)|
        act  = xsdbool( lv_single_count > 0 ) ).
      lv_combined_count = lv_combined_count + lv_single_count.
      ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all.
      ls_case-filter_raw = |PSTYPE eq '{ lv_type }'|.
      get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
        IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_combined) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_type_count) ).
      assert_fixture_window(
        is_case = ls_single_case i_json = lv_combined i_count = lv_type_count ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_all_pstype_request)|
        act  = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_combined ) > 0 ) ).
      assert_all_rows_match_filter(
        i_json     = lv_combined
        i_property = 'PSTYPE'
        i_value    = lv_type ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = lv_type_count
        exp  = lv_single_count
        msg  = |The all-PSTYPE request must retain the complete { lv_type } subset| ).
      CLEAR ls_case-filter_raw.
      FREE: lv_single, lv_combined.
    ENDLOOP.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count
      exp  = lv_combined_count
      msg  = 'All-PSTYPE count must equal material plus service, without losing a type' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD amount_format_x_y_default.
    reset_fixture( ).
    TYPES:
      BEGIN OF ts_format_case,
        amount         TYPE zcl_fi_das_utility=>t_amount_precision,
        decimal_format TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_decimal_format,
        grouping       TYPE abap_bool,
        expected       TYPE string,
      END OF ts_format_case,
      tt_format_case TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_format_case WITH EMPTY KEY.
    DATA(lt_cases) = VALUE tt_format_case(
      ( amount = '12345.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_true expected = `12,345.67` )
      ( amount = '12345.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_true expected = `12 345,67` )
      ( amount = '12345.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_true expected = `12.345,67` )
      ( amount = '123456789.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_true expected = `123 456 789,67` )
      ( amount = '123456789.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_false expected = `123456789,67` )
      ( amount = '123456789.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_true expected = `123,456,789.67` )
      ( amount = '123456789.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_true expected = `123.456.789,67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_true expected = `-12 345,67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_true expected = `-12,345.67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_true expected = `-12.345,67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_false expected = `-12345,67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_false expected = `-12345.67` )
      ( amount = '-12345.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_false expected = `-12345,67` )
      ( amount = '12345.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_false expected = `12345.67` )
      ( amount = '12345.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_false expected = `12345,67` )
      ( amount = '-123456789.67' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_true expected = `-123 456 789,67` )
      ( amount = '-123456789.67' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_true expected = `-123,456,789.67` )
      ( amount = '-123456789.67' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_true expected = `-123.456.789,67` )
      ( amount = '0' decimal_format = 'X' grouping = abap_true expected = `0.00` )
      ( amount = '0' decimal_format = space grouping = abap_true expected = `0,00` )
      ( amount = '0' decimal_format = 'Y' grouping = abap_true expected = `0,00` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_cases INTO DATA(ls_case).
      DATA(lv_amount_text) = |{ ls_case-amount NUMBER = RAW }|.
      mv_phase = 'Utility formatting / format_odata_amount'.
      mv_request = |amount={ lv_amount_text }; decimals=2; |
                && |format="{ ls_case-decimal_format }"; grouping="{ ls_case-grouping }"|.
      TRY.
      DATA(lv_actual) = zcl_fi_das_utility=>format_odata_amount(
        i_amount = ls_case-amount i_decimals = 2
        i_decimal_format = ls_case-decimal_format i_use_grouping = ls_case-grouping ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_format).
        DATA(lv_format_error) = exception_text( lx_format ).
        IF mv_difference_count > 0.
          lv_format_error = lv_format_error && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
                          && difference_text( 'Amount formatting contract failed.' ).
          mv_parity_reported = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = lv_format_error previous = lx_format.
      ENDTRY.
      IF lv_actual <> ls_case-expected.
        add_difference(
          |{ mv_group_test } (format_odata_amount): amount={ lv_amount_text }, |
            && |decimals=2, format="{ ls_case-decimal_format }", grouping="{ ls_case-grouping }"; |
            && |Expected="{ ls_case-expected }" (length={ strlen( ls_case-expected ) }); |
            && |Actual="{ lv_actual }" (length={ strlen( lv_actual ) }); |
            && |Check active utility c2bf1b3: group_integer RESPECTING BLANKS and numeric-input minus sign| ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    assert_no_differences( 'Amount formatting contract failed.' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

    " equals its configured threshold. These fixtures are not labelled as that boundary.
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      fixture = 'xsa-over-service.json' email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'
      flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over

    run_paged_case( ls_case ).

    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD review_day_boundary.
    reset_fixture( ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260906'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260906' )
      exp  = 'K'
      msg  = 'Day 6 before the review boundary must keep the reviewed status' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260906'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260907' )
      exp  = 'R'
      msg  = 'Crossing from day 6 to day 7 must reset the reviewed status' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = mo_cut->get_review_action(
              i_changed_date = '20260907'
              i_review_day   = 7
              i_current_date = '20260907' )
      exp  = 'K'
      msg  = 'A review performed on the review day must stay reviewed on that day' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD closed_pending_is_reviewed.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " XSA closure status takes precedence; this exposes a production discrepancy.
    DATA(ls_status) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_status(
      ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001'
      status = 'Pending' po_closure = 'true' ).
    DATA ls_result TYPE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_result.
    mo_cut->calculate_visible_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = ls_status i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ls_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_result-status exp = 'Reviewed'
      msg = 'Closure must take precedence over a persisted Pending status' ).
    CLEAR ls_status-status.
    CLEAR ls_result.
    mo_cut->calculate_visible_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = ls_status i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ls_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_result-status exp = 'Reviewed'
      msg = 'Closure must also take precedence over an initial persisted status' ).
    ls_status-status = 'Pending'.
    ls_status-po_closure = 'false'.
    CLEAR ls_result.
    mo_cut->calculate_visible_status(
      EXPORTING is_status = ls_status i_review_day = 7
      CHANGING cs_result = ls_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_result-status exp = 'Pending'
      msg = 'An open Pending row must not be changed to Reviewed' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD stored_method_read_contract.
    reset_fixture( ).
    mo_cut->mt_exchrt = VALUE #(
      ( source_curr = 'USD' target_curr = 'USD' exchange_rate = 1 ) ).
    DATA(ls_key) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_key(
      ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' pstype = '9' ).
    DATA(ls_context) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_context(
      review_day = 7
      details = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ls_key-ponumber itemno = ls_key-itemno effective_pstype = '9'
          gross_value = 1200 po_currency = 'USD' company_code_currency = 'USD'
          work_start_date = '20000101' work_end_date = '20001231'
          service_amount = '300' service_percentage = '25' ) )
      history = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ls_key-ponumber itemno = ls_key-itemno
          invoice_value = 20 invoice_local = 20 invoice_usd = 20 ) )
      statuses = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = ls_key-ponumber itemno = ls_key-itemno
          status = 'Pending' po_closure = 'true' accrual_method = 'WTD' wtd_amount = 999 ) ) ).
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      mode = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under ).
    TYPES: BEGIN OF ts_numbers,
             work TYPE decfloat34,
             work_usd TYPE decfloat34,
             percent TYPE decfloat34,
             accrued TYPE decfloat34,
             accrued_usd TYPE decfloat34,
           END OF ts_numbers.
    DATA(lt_methods) = VALUE tt_string( ( `WTD` ) ( `STL` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_methods INTO DATA(lv_method).
      ls_context-statuses[ ponumber = ls_key-ponumber itemno = ls_key-itemno ]-accrual_method = lv_method.
      DATA(ls_expected) = COND ts_numbers(
        WHEN lv_method = 'WTD' THEN VALUE #(
          work = 300 work_usd = 300 percent = 25 accrued = 280 accrued_usd = 280 )
        ELSE VALUE #(
          work = 1200 work_usd = 1200 percent = 100 accrued = 1180 accrued_usd = 1180 ) ).
      DO 2 TIMES.
        DATA(lv_closed) = xsdbool( sy-index = 1 ).
        ls_context-statuses[ ponumber = ls_key-ponumber itemno = ls_key-itemno ]-po_closure =
          COND #( WHEN lv_closed = abap_true THEN 'true' ELSE 'false' ).
        DATA(ls_row) = mo_cut->get_row(
          is_key = ls_key is_context = ls_context is_request = ls_request ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act = VALUE ts_numbers(
            work = ls_row-totwrkcompamt work_usd = ls_row-totwrkcompamt_usd
            percent = ls_row-totwrkcompper accrued = ls_row-amountaccrued
            accrued_usd = ls_row-amountaccrued_usd )
          exp = ls_expected
          msg = |Under Service stored method={ lv_method }; closed={ lv_closed }; |
              && |USD rate=1, completed PO=1200, service work=300, invoice=20; |
              && |closure must not zero the read formula| ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_row-accrualmethod exp = lv_method ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_row-straightlinemthd exp = xsdbool( lv_method = 'STL' ) ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_row-edgewtdamt exp = 999
          msg = 'Stored edge WTD is distinct from the calculated work amount' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_row-po_closure exp = lv_closed ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_row-poclosure_flag
          exp = COND i( WHEN lv_closed = abap_true THEN 1 ELSE 0 ) ).
      ENDDO.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD observed_po_item_or.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " Supplied Gateway observation, not an invocation of Gateway translation.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #(
        filter_string = `(PONUMBER = '8000401022') OR (ITEMNO = '00001')` ) ).
    DATA(lv_predicate) = ls_request-filter-filter_string.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ).
    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter_for_amdp ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter-filter_string exp = lv_predicate ).
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00002' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002' ) ).
    DATA(lt_expected) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001' original_row_index = 1 )
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00002' original_row_index = 2 )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' original_row_index = 3 ) ).
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_result exp = lt_expected
      msg = 'OR must retain both one-sided witnesses and reject the neither row' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD observed_po_item_and.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " Supplied Gateway observation: AND has complete populated select-options.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #(
        filter_string = `(PONUMBER = '8000401022') AND (ITEMNO = '00001')`
        filter_select_options = VALUE #(
          ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000401022' )
          ( property = 'ITEMNO' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '00001' ) ) ) ).
    DATA(lt_ranges) = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options.
    DATA(lv_predicate) = ls_request-filter-filter_string.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lt_ranges ) exp = 2 ).
    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter_for_amdp ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_request-filter-filter_string exp = lv_predicate ).
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00002' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00002' ) ).
    DATA(lt_optimized) = lt_result.
    DATA(lt_expected) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001' original_row_index = 1 ) ).
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_result exp = lt_expected
      msg = 'AND must reject both one-sided witnesses and the neither row' ).
    " The same complete, representable ranges also exercise the select-only path.
    DATA(ls_optimized) = extract_range( lt_ranges ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_optimized-filter_for_amdp-ponumber ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_optimized-filter_for_amdp-item ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = ls_optimized-filter-filter_string ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_optimized-filter_for_amdp-ponumber
                 CHANGING ct_result = lt_optimized ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_optimized-filter_for_amdp-item
                 CHANGING ct_result = lt_optimized ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_optimized exp = lt_expected
      msg = 'Optimized complete AND ranges must retain exactly the same PO/item' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD missing_predicate_fails.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lt_denied) = VALUE tt_string( ( `NOT_A_COLUMN` ) ( `ORIGINAL_ROW_INDEX` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_denied INTO DATA(lv_property).
      DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
        filter = VALUE #( filter_select_options = VALUE #(
          ( property = lv_property sign = `I` option = `EQ` low = `1000` ) ) ) ).
      TRY.
          mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            msg  = |Unsupported or internal range property { lv_property } must be rejected| ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (missing_predicate_fails)|
            act  = lx_error->reason ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD empty_ranges_clear_stale.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter_for_amdp = VALUE #(
        ponumber = `PONUMBER = 'stale'` item = `ITEMNO = 'stale'`
        suppliername = `SUPPLIERNAME = 'stale'` ) ).

      act  = ls_request-filter_for_amdp ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (empty_ranges_clear_stale)|
      act  = ls_request-filter ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options
      msg  = 'Empty range extraction must consume every projection' ).
    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).

      quit = mv_assert_quit

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (empty_ranges_clear_stale)|
      act  = mo_cut->is_full_processing_required( ls_request ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD key_ranges_replace_stale.

      act  = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD company_range_hybrid_parity.

      filter = VALUE #(
        filter_string         = `COMPANYCODE = '1000'`

          ( property = 'COMPANYCODE' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '1000' ) ) ) ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (company_range_hybrid_parity)|
      act  = ls_hybrid_request-filter-filter_string
      exp  = `COMPANYCODE = '1000'` ).
    DATA(lt_range) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' itemno = '00001' companycode = '1000' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' companycode = '2000' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000003' itemno = '00001' companycode = '1000' ) ).
    DATA(lt_hybrid) = lt_range.
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_range_request CHANGING ct_result = lt_range ).
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_hybrid_request CHANGING ct_result = lt_hybrid ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(

      exp  = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (company_range_hybrid_parity)|
      act  = lt_range[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp  = '8000000001' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (company_range_hybrid_parity)|
      act  = lt_range[ 2 ]-ponumber
      exp  = '8000000003' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lt_range
      exp  = lt_hybrid
      msg  = 'Converted select-only COMPANYCODE ranges must match the complete hybrid predicate' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD po_group_and_supplier_rows.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " Select-options OR values within a property, but AND different properties.
    DATA(ls_request) = extract_range( VALUE #(
      ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000401022' )
      ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000266248' )
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' ) ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows): PO predicate|
      act  = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows): Supplier predicate|
      act  = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows): Optimized ranges|
      act  = ls_request-filter-filter_string ).
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' )
      ( ponumber = '8000266248' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fujifilm' )
      ( ponumber = '8000401022' itemno = '00002' suppliername = 'Other Supplier' )
      ( ponumber = '8000000002' itemno = '00001' suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ) ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber
                 CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername
                 CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows): Both properties must match|
      act  = lines( lt_result )
      exp  = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows)|
      act  = lt_result[ 1 ]-ponumber
      exp  = '8000401022' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows)|
      act  = lt_result[ 2 ]-ponumber
      exp  = '8000266248' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (po_group_and_supplier_rows)|
      act  = lt_result[ 2 ]-suppliername
      exp  = 'Fujifilm' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_unknown_prop_no_dump.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " Unknown SQL columns are rejected, not silently discarded.
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '8000000001' ) ( ponumber = '8000000002' ) ).
    TRY.
        filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = `NOT_A_COLUMN = 'X'`
                     CHANGING ct_result    = lt_result ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = 'Invalid generic column must raise the domain exception' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_bound(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (generic_unknown_prop_no_dump)|
          act  = lx_error->previous ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (generic_unknown_prop_no_dump)|
          act  = xsdbool( lx_error->previous IS INSTANCE OF cx_amdp_error ) ).
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD generic_with_sort_and_paging.

  METHOD test_eq_case_insensitive.
    reset_fixture( ).
    " Extracted EQ is case-sensitive; explicit UPPER below is not the default EQ behavior.
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( suppliername = 'Fuji' ) ( suppliername = 'fuji' )
      ( suppliername = 'FUJI' ) ( suppliername = 'Hitachi' ) ).
    DATA(lt_exact) = lt_result.
    DATA(ls_request) = extract_range( VALUE #(
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 'FUJI' ) ) ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername
                 CHANGING ct_result    = lt_exact ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_eq_case_insensitive)|
      act  = lines( lt_exact )
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_eq_case_insensitive)|
      act  = lt_exact[ 1 ]-suppliername
      exp  = 'FUJI' ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = `UPPER(SUPPLIERNAME) = 'FUJI'`
                 CHANGING ct_result    = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_eq_case_insensitive)|
      act  = lines( lt_result )
      exp  = 3 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_eq_case_insensitive)|
      act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_result[ suppliername = 'Hitachi' ] ) ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.

      exp  = '8000000002' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (generic_preserves_row_order)|
      act  = lt_result[ 1 ]-original_row_index
      exp  = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (generic_preserves_row_order)|
      act  = lt_result[ 2 ]-original_row_index
      exp  = 3 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD complex_filter_hana.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lv_predicate) =
      `(POLINEDESC NOT LIKE '%b%' AND PONUMBER NOT LIKE '%5%') `
      && `AND POLINEDESC LIKE '%a%' AND POCREATEDAT > '20251014' `
      && `AND (SUPPLIERNAME LIKE 'B%' OR SUPPLIERNAME LIKE '%A%') `
      && `AND (PONUMBER <= '977' OR PONUMBER > '9000600000' `
      && `OR (PONUMBER >= '8000006000' AND PONUMBER <= '8000906000') `
      && `OR PONUMBER = '8000096102' OR PONUMBER = '8000097224') `
      && `AND (ITEMNO = '00001' OR ITEMNO = '00003' OR ITEMNO = '00004')`.
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( ponumber = '0000000010' itemno = '00001' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' )
      ( ponumber = '9999999999' itemno = '00003' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'XAY' )
      ( ponumber = '8000006000' itemno = '00004' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' )
      ( ponumber = '8000906000' itemno = '00001' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' )
      ( ponumber = '8000096102' itemno = '00003' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' )
      ( ponumber = '8000097224' itemno = '00004' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' )
      ( ponumber = '8001984177' itemno = '00001' polinedesc = 'a'
        pocreatedat = '20251015' suppliername = 'Beta' ) ).
    DATA(lt_expected) = lt_result.
    DATA(ls_rejected) = lt_result[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-polinedesc = 'ab'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-ponumber = '8000000005'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-polinedesc = 'A'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-pocreatedat = '20251014'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-suppliername = 'beta'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-itemno = '00002'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    ls_rejected = lt_expected[ 1 ].
    ls_rejected-pocreatedat = '20251013'.
    APPEND ls_rejected TO lt_result.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #(
        filter_string         = lv_predicate
        filter_select_options = VALUE #(
          ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = '8000096102' ) ) ) ).
    mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
      act  = ls_request-filter_for_amdp ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
      act  = ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
      act  = ls_request-filter-filter_string
      exp  = lv_predicate ).
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
      act  = lines( lt_result )
      exp  = lines( lt_expected ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_expected INTO DATA(ls_expected).
      DATA(lv_index) = sy-tabix.
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
        act  = lt_result[ lv_index ]-ponumber
        exp  = ls_expected-ponumber ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
        act  = lt_result[ lv_index ]-itemno
        exp  = ls_expected-itemno ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (complex_filter_hana)|
        act  = lt_result[ lv_index ]-original_row_index
        exp  = lv_index ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA ls_case TYPE ts_case.

    ls_case-fixture =
      'xsa-filter.json'.

    ls_case-email =
      'patricia-tiemi.oshiro@takeda.com'.

    ls_case-flow =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.

    ls_case-pstype =
      zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    run_paged_case(
      is_case = ls_case ).

    assert_no_differences( ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_companycode.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com' flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
      filter_raw = `COMPANYCODE eq '2028'`
      top = 100 top_specified = abap_true inlinecount = abap_true ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_json) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_companycode)|
      act  = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_json ) > 0 ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_companycode)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count >= get_result_count( lv_json ) ) ).
    assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_json
                                  i_property = 'COMPANYCODE'
                                  i_value    = '2028' ).
    assert_fixture_window(
      is_case = ls_case i_json = lv_json i_count = lv_count
      i_property = 'COMPANYCODE' i_value = '2028' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_curr_company.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com' flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
      filter_raw = `POCURRENCY eq 'JPY' and COMPANYCODE eq '2780'`
      top = 100 top_specified = abap_true inlinecount = abap_true ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_json) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_curr_company)|
      act  = xsdbool( get_result_count( lv_json ) > 0 ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_curr_company)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count >= get_result_count( lv_json ) ) ).
    assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_json
                                  i_property = 'POCURRENCY'
                                  i_value    = 'JPY' ).
    assert_all_rows_match_filter( i_json     = lv_json
                                  i_property = 'COMPANYCODE'
                                  i_value    = '2780' ).
    assert_fixture_window(
      is_case = ls_case i_json = lv_json i_count = lv_count
      i_property = 'POCURRENCY' i_value = 'JPY'
      i_property_2 = 'COMPANYCODE' i_value_2 = '2780' ).
    ls_case-filter_raw = `POCURRENCY eq 'JPY' and COMPANYCODE eq '2028'`.
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = lv_json e_inline_count = lv_count ).
    assert_fixture_window(
      is_case = ls_case i_json = lv_json i_count = lv_count
      i_property = 'POCURRENCY' i_value = 'JPY'
      i_property_2 = 'COMPANYCODE' i_value_2 = '2028' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lv_count exp = 0
      msg = 'JPY/2028 is a zero-row witness in the supplied snapshot, not a positive fixture' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD test_filter_po_or_supplier.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case      TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json      TYPE string,
      lv_count     TYPE int8,
      lv_row_count TYPE i,
      lv_index     TYPE i.

    ls_case-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case-filter_raw =
      `(PONUMBER eq '8002077142' or PONUMBER eq '8002103777') `
      && `and substringof('Hitachi',SUPPLIERNAME)`.
    ls_case-top = 100.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case-inlinecount = abap_true.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json
        e_inline_count = lv_count ).

    lv_row_count = get_result_count( lv_json ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( lv_row_count > 0 )
      msg  = 'Combined OR/AND filter must return at least one live row' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (test_filter_po_or_supplier)|
      act  = xsdbool( lv_count >= lv_row_count ) ).

    assert_all_rows_match_filter(
      i_json     = lv_json
      i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME'
      i_value    = 'Hitachi'
      i_contains = abap_true ).

    WHILE lv_index < lv_row_count.
      lv_index = lv_index + 1.

      DATA(lv_ponumber) =
        read_result_property(
          i_json     = lv_json
          i_index    = lv_index
          i_property = 'PONUMBER' ).

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = xsdbool(
          lv_ponumber = '8002077142'
          OR lv_ponumber = '8002103777' )
        msg  = |Row { lv_index } must belong to one of the requested PO numbers| ).
    ENDWHILE.
    assert_fixture_window(
      is_case = ls_case i_json = lv_json i_count = lv_count
      i_property = 'PONUMBER' i_value = '8002077142' i_alternative = '8002103777'
      i_property_2 = 'SUPPLIERNAME' i_value_2 = 'Hitachi' i_contains_2 = abap_true ).

  ENDMETHOD.
