  METHOD test_empty_contains_po.
    reset_fixture( ).

    DATA:
      ls_case_base   TYPE ts_case,
      ls_case_empty  TYPE ts_case,
      lv_json_base   TYPE string,
      lv_json_empty  TYPE string,
      lv_count_base  TYPE int8,
      lv_count_empty TYPE int8,
      lt_base_index  TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_empty_index TYPE tt_expected_index.

    ls_case_base-email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'.
    ls_case_base-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case_base-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
    ls_case_base-top = 10.
    ls_case_base-top_specified = abap_true.
    ls_case_base-inlinecount = abap_true.

    ls_case_empty = ls_case_base.
    ls_case_empty-filter_raw = `substringof('',PONUMBER)`.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_base
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_base
        e_inline_count = lv_count_base ).

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = ls_case_empty
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = lv_json_empty
        e_inline_count = lv_count_empty ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_count_empty
      exp  = lv_count_base
      msg  = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same inline count as no filter' ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = get_result_count( lv_json_empty )
      exp  = get_result_count( lv_json_base )
      msg  = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same page size as no filter' ).

    lt_base_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_json_base ).

    lt_empty_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_json_empty ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lines( lt_empty_index )
      exp  = lines( lt_base_index )
      msg  = 'Empty substring filter must keep the same row-key set size' ).

    LOOP AT lt_empty_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_empty_row>).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = xsdbool(
          line_exists(
            lt_base_index[
              ponumber = <ls_empty_row>-ponumber
              itemno   = <ls_empty_row>-itemno ] ) )
        msg  = 'Empty substring filter must not change the returned row keys' ).
    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD complex_filter_gateway.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      fixture = 'xsa-over-service.json' email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'
      flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
      filter_raw =
        `(not substringof('b',POLINEDESC) and not substringof('5',PONUMBER)) `
        && `and substringof('a',POLINEDESC) `
        && `and POCREATEDAT gt datetime'2025-10-14T00:00:00' `
        && `and (startswith(SUPPLIERNAME,'B') or substringof('A',SUPPLIERNAME)) `
        && `and (PONUMBER le '977' or PONUMBER gt '9000600000' `
        && `or (PONUMBER ge '8000006000' and PONUMBER le '8000906000') `
        && `or PONUMBER eq '8000096102' or PONUMBER eq '8000097224') `
        && `and (ITEMNO eq '00001' or ITEMNO eq '00003' or ITEMNO eq '00004')` ).
    run_paged_case( is_case          = ls_case
                    i_complex_filter = abap_true ).
    assert_no_differences( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD percentage_parsing_and_error.
    reset_fixture( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (percentage_parsing_and_error)|
      act  = zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( i_value = `12,5` )
      exp  = CONV zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>t_percentage( '12.5' ) ).
    TRY.
        zcl_fi_das_utility=>parse_percentage( i_value = `1,2.3` ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = `Mixed decimal separators must be rejected` ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (percentage_parsing_and_error)|
          act  = lx_error->reason ).
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD json_oracle_contract.
    reset_fixture( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\\n"' ) exp = '\n'
      msg = 'A literal backslash plus n is not a newline' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\n"' )
      exp = CONV string( cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\\u0026"' ) exp = '\u0026' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\u0026\u00e9\u0020"' ) exp = `&é ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\ud83d\ude00"' ) exp = '😀' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\\\""' ) exp = '\"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;;B;A' i_actual = 'A;A;;B' ) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = equal_aggregate_content(
        i_expected = 'A;;B;A' i_actual = 'A;;B' ) ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'GLACCOUNT' i_expected_token = '"B;A;A"' i_actual_token = '"A;B;A"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 0 ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'GLACCOUNT' i_expected_token = 'null' i_actual_token = '"null"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 1
      msg = 'Aggregate allowances must not erase JSON token types' ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'GLACCOUNT' i_expected_token = '123' i_actual_token = '"123"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 2 ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'POLINEDESC' i_expected_token = '"\\n"' i_actual_token = '"\n"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 3 ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'FULL_NAME' i_expected_token = 'null' i_actual_token = '""' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 3
      msg = 'The explicit one-way field-specific null allowance is retained' ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'synthetic' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '1'
      i_name = 'FULL_NAME' i_expected_token = '""' i_actual_token = 'null' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = mv_difference_count exp = 4 ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD json_parser_contract.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lv_valid) =
      `{"noise":{"__count":"999","results":[]},"d":{`
      && `"nested":{"__count":"888","results":[]},"__count":"2","results":[`
      && `{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","SUPPLIERNAME":"\"Z\"","VALUES":[1,null,{"A":true}]},`
      && `{"PONUMBER":"2","ITEMNO":"1","SUPPLIERNAME":"A"}]}}`.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = get_result_count( lv_valid ) exp = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = read_inline_count( lv_valid ) exp = 2 ).
    DATA(lt_keys) = build_expected_index( lv_valid ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lt_keys ) exp = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = read_result_property(
        i_json = lv_valid i_index = 1 i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' )
      exp = '"Z"' ).
    assert_rows_sorted( i_json = lv_valid i_property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ).
    DATA(lt_invalid) = VALUE tt_string(
      ( `{"d":{"results":[,]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1"},]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1" "ITEMNO":"1"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":01}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":"\q"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":"\ud83d"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":"\ude00"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","A":[1,]}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":null,"ITEMNO":"1"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1","PONUMBER":"2"}]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1"},{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"1"}]}}` )
      ( `{"error":{"message":"not an OData result"}}` )
      ( `{"error":{"__count":"0","results":[]}}` )
      ( `{"results":[]}` )
      ( `{"d":[]}` )
      ( `{"d":{"nested":{"results":[]}}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"__count":"0","__count":"1","results":[]}}` )
      ( `{"d":{"results":[]}} trailing` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_invalid INTO DATA(lv_invalid).
      TRY.
          lt_keys = build_expected_index( lv_invalid ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
            quit = mv_assert_quit msg = |Malformed/duplicate response was accepted: { lv_invalid }| ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_error).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
            quit = mv_assert_quit act = lx_error->reason ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
    TRY.
        DATA(lv_count) = read_inline_count(
          `{"d":{"nested":{"__count":"0"},"results":[]}}` ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Missing requested count must not masquerade as zero' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO lx_error.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
          quit = mv_assert_quit act = lx_error->reason ).
    ENDTRY.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = normalize_odata_time( '"PT9H4M1S"' )
      exp = '"PT09H04M01S"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = normalize_odata_time( '"PT999999999999999999H0M0S"' )
      exp = '"PT999999999999999999H0M0S"' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = normalize_odata_time( 'PT9H4M1S' ) exp = 'PT9H4M1S' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD range_sql_witnesses.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(ls_request) = extract_range( VALUE #(
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = '*Fuji*' )
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'EQ' low = 'Hitachi' )
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'E' option = 'EQ' low = 'Fuji Electric' )
      ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'E' option = 'CP' low = '*Lab*' ) ) ).
    DATA(lt_result) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
      ( suppliername = 'Fuji Electric' ) ( suppliername = 'Fuji Lab' )
      ( suppliername = 'Fujifilm' ) ( suppliername = 'Hitachi' )
      ( suppliername = 'Other' ) ).
    filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername
                 CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_result
      exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
        ( suppliername = 'Fujifilm' original_row_index = 3 )
        ( suppliername = 'Hitachi' original_row_index = 4 ) )
      msg = 'Include union must be intersected with every exclusion' ).
    DO 2 TIMES.
      DATA(lv_sign) = COND string( WHEN sy-index = 1 THEN 'I' ELSE 'E' ).
      ls_request = extract_range( VALUE #(
        ( property = 'PONUMBER' sign = lv_sign option = 'BT'
          low = '8000000002' high = '8000000004' ) ) ).
      lt_result = VALUE #(
        ( ponumber = '8000000001' ) ( ponumber = '8000000002' )
        ( ponumber = '8000000003' ) ( ponumber = '8000000004' )
        ( ponumber = '8000000005' ) ).
      filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber
                   CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
      DATA(lt_expected) = COND zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
        WHEN lv_sign = 'I' THEN VALUE #(
          ( ponumber = '8000000002' original_row_index = 2 )
          ( ponumber = '8000000003' original_row_index = 3 )
          ( ponumber = '8000000004' original_row_index = 4 ) )
        ELSE VALUE #(
          ( ponumber = '8000000001' original_row_index = 1 )
          ( ponumber = '8000000005' original_row_index = 5 ) ) ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_result exp = lt_expected
        msg = 'BT boundaries are inclusive; exclusion is the exact complement' ).
    ENDDO.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF ts_pattern,
             pattern TYPE string,
             matching TYPE string,
             rejecting TYPE string,
           END OF ts_pattern.
    DATA lt_patterns TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ts_pattern WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_patterns = VALUE #(
      ( pattern = '*100%*' matching = '100% done' rejecting = '1000 done' )
      ( pattern = '*A_B*' matching = 'A_B' rejecting = 'AXB' )
      ( pattern = `*O'Brien*` matching = `O'Brien` rejecting = 'OBrien' )
      ( pattern = '*A#*B*' matching = 'A*B' rejecting = 'AxxxB' )
      ( pattern = '*A#+B*' matching = 'A+B' rejecting = 'AxB' ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_patterns INTO DATA(ls_pattern).
      ls_request = extract_range( VALUE #(
        ( property = 'SUPPLIERNAME' sign = 'I' option = 'CP' low = ls_pattern-pattern ) ) ).
      lt_result = VALUE #(
        ( suppliername = ls_pattern-matching ) ( suppliername = ls_pattern-rejecting ) ).
      filter_rows( EXPORTING i_predicate = ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername
                   CHANGING ct_result = lt_result ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_result
        exp = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>tt_odata_result(
          ( suppliername = ls_pattern-matching original_row_index = 1 ) )
        msg = |Literal SQL/CP escaping must preserve { ls_pattern-pattern }| ).
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD diagnostic_contract.
    reset_fixture( ).
    DATA(lo_native_probe) = NEW ltc_parity( ).
    lo_native_probe->diagnostic_good_leaf( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_native_probe->mv_group_test exp = 'diagnostic_good_leaf'
      msg = 'Native reset must identify the actual caller, not a framework setup frame' ).
    DATA(lo_probe) = NEW ltc_parity( ).
    DATA lt_log TYPE stringtab.
    lo_probe->mr_failure_log = REF #( lt_log ).
    lo_probe->mv_group_test = 'native_diagnostic_leaf'.
    lo_probe->diagnostic_bad_leaf( ).
    DATA(lv_expected) =
      `DAS XSA vs SEGW OData parity failed. Difference count: 2.`
      && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
      && `--- First 2 difference(s) ---`
      && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
      && `String value differs; Fixture=xsa-over-service.json; PO=8002122797; ITEMNO=00001; `
      && `Field=FULL_NAME; XSA="Charlotte Engel Møller"; SEGW=""`
      && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
      && `String value differs; Fixture=xsa-over-service.json; PO=8002139944; ITEMNO=00001; `
      && `Field=FULL_NAME; XSA="Charlotte Engel Møller"; SEGW=""`.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_log exp = VALUE stringtab( ( lv_expected ) )
      msg = 'Native reporting must retain the complete original parity diagnostic' ).
    CLEAR lt_log.
    lo_probe->run_group_test( 'diagnostic_bad_leaf' ).
    lo_probe->run_group_test( 'diagnostic_good_leaf' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_log exp = VALUE stringtab( ( lv_expected ) )
      msg = 'A successful later leaf must neither erase nor duplicate the grouped failure' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_probe->mv_unused exp = abap_true
      msg = 'The successful leaf after a failure must actually run' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_probe->mv_group_test exp = 'native_diagnostic_leaf' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_probe->mv_assert_quit exp = if_aunit_constants=>method ).

    CLEAR lt_log.
    lo_probe->run_group_test( 'diagnostic_domain_leaf' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lt_log ) exp = 1
      msg = 'A domain failure must produce one nonblank report, not a parity mismatch' ).
    DATA(lv_domain) = lt_log[ 1 ].
    DATA(lt_required) = VALUE tt_string(
      ( `Test=diagnostic_domain_leaf` ) ( `Fixture=broken.json` )
      ( `Phase=EXPECTED fixture indexing` ) ( `Request=fixture MIME` )
      ( `ZCX_FI_DAS_ERROR` ) ( `outer domain reason` )
      ( `inner domain reason` ) ( `Source=` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_required INTO DATA(lv_required).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit act = xsdbool( lv_domain CS lv_required )
        msg = |Domain report lost "{ lv_required }": { lv_domain }| ).
    ENDLOOP.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = xsdbool( lv_domain CS 'String value differs' )
      msg = 'A parser failure cannot be fabricated as a field mismatch' ).
    DATA(lo_blank) = NEW zcx_fi_das_error( ).
    DATA(lv_blank) = lo_probe->exception_text( lo_blank ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = xsdbool( lv_blank CS 'Reason=' AND lv_blank CS 'ZCX_FI_DAS_ERROR' )
      msg = 'Even an exception without a reason must identify its class and text fallback' ).
    DATA lo_silent TYPE REF TO cx_root.
    lo_silent = NEW lcx_silent_error( i_reason = 'empty get_text domain reason' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_silent->get_text( )
      msg = 'Arrange an exception whose get_text is actually empty' ).
    DATA(lo_wrapped) = NEW zcx_fi_das_error(
      i_reason = 'wrapped failure' previous = lo_silent ).
    DATA(lv_wrapped) = lo_probe->exception_text( lo_wrapped ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = xsdbool( lv_wrapped CS 'wrapped failure'
                  AND lv_wrapped CS 'empty get_text domain reason'
                  AND lv_wrapped CS 'LCX_SILENT_ERROR' )
      msg = 'A wrapped exception with empty get_text must retain the previous domain reason' ).
    DATA lo_native TYPE REF TO cx_root.
    lo_native = NEW lcx_parity_error( i_reason = lv_wrapped previous = lo_wrapped ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_native->get_text( ) exp = lv_wrapped
      msg = 'Native helper exceptions must expose the same complete diagnostic as grouped ones' ).

    lo_probe->reset_fixture( ).
    DO mc_max_log + 3 TIMES.
      lo_probe->add_difference( |Mismatch { sy-index }| ).
    ENDDO.
    DATA(lv_bounded) = lo_probe->difference_text( ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lo_probe->mv_difference_count exp = mc_max_log + 3 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lo_probe->mt_differences ) exp = mc_max_log ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = xsdbool( lv_bounded CS |Difference count: { mc_max_log + 3 }.|
                  AND lv_bounded CS |--- First { mc_max_log } difference(s) ---|
                  AND lv_bounded CS '--- 3 further difference(s) omitted ---' )
      msg = 'The sample bound must not become the total mismatch count' ).
    lo_probe->reset_fixture( ).
    lo_probe->compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'format.json' i_ponumber = '1' i_itemno = '00001'
      i_name = 'AMOUNT' i_expected_token = `"-12 345,67 "` i_actual_token = `"12345,67"` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = xsdbool( lo_probe->difference_text( ) CS `XSA="-12 345,67 "; SEGW="12345,67"` )
      msg = 'Diagnostics must preserve spaces, minus signs and original JSON tokens' ).
    DATA(lv_format_report) = lo_probe->difference_text( 'Amount formatting contract failed.' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = xsdbool( lv_format_report CS 'Amount formatting contract failed. Difference count: 1.'
                  AND lv_format_report CS `XSA="-12 345,67 "; SEGW="12345,67"` )
      msg = 'Formatting reports must retain exact tokens without claiming an OData comparison' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = lo_probe->bounded_text( i_text = repeat( val = 'A' occ = 1000 ) i_limit = 8 )
      exp = 'AAAAAAAA...[length=1000]'
      msg = 'Oversized contextual values must be bounded and declare their original length' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD fixture_encoding_contract.
    reset_fixture( ).
    mv_fixture = 'bom-fixture.json'.
    DATA(lv_json) = decode_fixture(
      'EFBBBF7B2264223A7B22726573756C7473223A5B7B22504F4E554D424552223A2231222C224954454D4E4F223A223030303031227D5D7D7D' ).
    DATA(lv_expected) = `{"d":{"results":[{"PONUMBER":"1","ITEMNO":"00001"}]}}`.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lv_json exp = lv_expected
      msg = 'Read legitimate UTF-8 MIME fixtures with exactly one leading BOM' ).
    DATA(lt_index) = build_expected_index( lv_json ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lt_index ) exp = 1 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = xsdbool( line_exists(
        lt_index[ ponumber = '1' itemno = '00001' ] ) ) ).
    DATA(lv_bom) = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>uccpi( 65279 ).
    CONCATENATE lv_bom(1) lv_expected INTO lv_json RESPECTING BLANKS.
    lt_index = build_expected_index( lv_json ).
    DATA(ls_index) = lt_index[ ponumber = '1' itemno = '00001' ].
    DATA(lt_properties) = read_object_properties(
      i_json = lv_json i_object_from = ls_index-object_from i_object_to = ls_index-object_to ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lt_properties[ name = 'ITEMNO' ]-token exp = '"00001"'
      msg = 'Converter-retained BOM must not invalidate stored character offsets' ).
    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture indexing'.
    TRY.
        lt_index = build_expected_index( `{broken}` ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Malformed fixtures must still fail' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_bad).
        DATA(lv_details) = lx_bad->get_text( ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act = xsdbool( lv_details CS 'find_odata_property' AND lv_details CS 'offset=1'
                      AND lv_details CS 'length=8' AND lv_details CS 'EXPECTED fixture indexing'
                      AND lv_details CS 'bom-fixture.json' )
          msg = |Malformed fixture must expose its phase and exact parser offset: { lv_details }| ).
    ENDTRY.
    TRY.
        lv_json = decode_fixture( 'EFBBBFFF' ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Invalid UTF-8 cannot be replaced silently' ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO lx_bad.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act = xsdbool( lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'decode_fixture'
                      AND lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'bytes=4' ) ).
    ENDTRY.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\u0020"' ) exp = ` `
      msg = 'U+0020 alone must remain a string of length one, not cause substring overflow' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = strlen( unquote_json( '"\u0020\u0020"' ) ) exp = 2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"A\u0020B\u0020"' ) exp = `A B ` ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = unquote_json( '"\ud83d\ude00\u0020"' ) exp = `😀 `
      msg = 'UTF-16 surrogate pair and trailing space must survive the same decoder' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = strlen( unquote_json( '"\u0000\b\f"' ) ) exp = 3
      msg = 'Decoded control characters must each occupy one UTF-16 unit' ).
    DATA(lt_bad_tokens) = VALUE tt_string(
      ( `"\u"` ) ( `"\u002"` ) ( `"\ud83d"` ) ( `"\ude00"` ) ( `"\ud83d\u0020"` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_bad_tokens INTO DATA(lv_bad_token).
      TRY.
          DATA(lv_decoded) = unquote_json( lv_bad_token ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
            quit = mv_assert_quit msg = |Malformed Unicode accepted: { lv_bad_token }| ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO lx_bad.
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            act = xsdbool( lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'Helper=unquote_json'
                        AND lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'offset='
                        AND lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'token length=' ) ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
    DATA lt_bad_offsets TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF i WITH EMPTY KEY.
    lt_bad_offsets = VALUE #( ( -1 ) ( 3 ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_bad_offsets INTO DATA(lv_bad_offset).
      TRY.
          DATA(lv_position) = skip_whitespace( i_text = '[]' i_position = lv_bad_offset ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Invalid whitespace offset was accepted' ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO lx_bad.
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            act = xsdbool( lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'Helper=skip_whitespace'
                        AND lx_bad->get_text( ) CS |offset={ lv_bad_offset }| ) ).
      ENDTRY.
      TRY.
          lv_position = find_matching_end(
            i_text = '[]' i_start = lv_bad_offset i_open = '[' i_close = ']' ).
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Invalid container offset was accepted' ).
        CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO lx_bad.
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            act = xsdbool( lx_bad->get_text( ) CS 'Helper=find_matching_end'
                        AND lx_bad->get_text( ) CS |offset={ lv_bad_offset }| ) ).
      ENDTRY.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD diagnostic_bad_leaf.
    reset_fixture( ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'xsa-over-service.json' i_ponumber = '8002122797' i_itemno = '00001'
      i_name = 'FULL_NAME' i_expected_token = `"Charlotte Engel Møller"` i_actual_token = `""` ).
    compare_raw_property(
      i_fixture = 'xsa-over-service.json' i_ponumber = '8002139944' i_itemno = '00001'
      i_name = 'FULL_NAME' i_expected_token = `"Charlotte Engel Møller"` i_actual_token = `""` ).
    assert_no_differences( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD diagnostic_good_leaf.
    reset_fixture( ).
    mv_unused = abap_true.
    assert_no_differences( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD diagnostic_domain_leaf.
    reset_fixture( ).
    mv_fixture = 'broken.json'.
    mv_request = 'fixture MIME'.
    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture indexing'.
    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      EXPORTING i_reason = 'outer domain reason'
        previous = NEW zcx_fi_das_error( i_reason = 'inner domain reason' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD run_group_test.
    DATA(lv_previous_quit) = mv_assert_quit.
    DATA(lv_previous_test) = mv_group_test.
    mv_assert_quit = if_aunit_constants=>method.
    mv_group_test = i_test.
    DATA(lv_method) = to_upper( i_test ).
    TRY.
        CALL METHOD me->(lv_method).
      CATCH cx_root INTO DATA(lx_error).
        " ABAP Unit registers the original assertion before its quit exception.
        " Do not turn that control flow into a second, empty assertion.
        IF is_framework_quit( lx_error ) = abap_false.
          DATA(lv_error_text) = COND string(
            WHEN lx_error IS INSTANCE OF lcx_parity_error THEN lx_error->get_text( )
            ELSE exception_text( lx_error ) ).
          IF mv_difference_count > 0 AND mv_parity_reported = abap_false.
            lv_error_text = lv_error_text && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
                         && difference_text( ).
          ENDIF.
          mv_assert_quit = if_aunit_constants=>no.
          report_failure( lv_error_text ).
        ENDIF.
    ENDTRY.
    mv_assert_quit = lv_previous_quit.
    mv_group_test = lv_previous_test.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD bounded_text.
    rv_text = i_text.
    IF strlen( i_text ) > i_limit.
      rv_text = substring( val = i_text len = i_limit )
             && |...[length={ strlen( i_text ) }]|.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD is_framework_quit.
    DATA(lv_class) = cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_object_ref( i_error )->absolute_name.
    rv_quit = xsdbool( lv_class = '\CLASS=CX_AUNIT_SBX_QUIT_TEST_METHOD' ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD exception_text.
    rv_text = |Test={ mv_group_test }; Fixture={ mv_fixture }; |
           && |Request={ mv_request }; Phase={ mv_phase }|.
    DATA(lo_error) = i_error.
    DO 8 TIMES.
      IF lo_error IS NOT BOUND.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      DATA(lv_class) = cl_abap_typedescr=>describe_by_object_ref( lo_error )->absolute_name.
      DATA(lv_reason) = lo_error->get_text( ).
      IF lo_error IS INSTANCE OF zcx_fi_das_error.
        DATA(lo_domain) = CAST zcx_fi_das_error( lo_error ).
        IF lo_domain->reason IS NOT INITIAL.
          lv_reason = lo_domain->reason.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      IF lv_reason IS INITIAL.
        lv_reason = 'No exception text supplied; use class and source position'.
      ENDIF.
      lo_error->get_source_position(
        IMPORTING include_name = DATA(lv_include) source_line = DATA(lv_line) ).
      rv_text = rv_text && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
              && |Exception[{ sy-index }]={ lv_class }; |
              && |Reason={ bounded_text( i_text = lv_reason i_limit = 2048 ) }; |
              && |Source={ lv_include }:{ lv_line }|.
      lo_error = lo_error->previous.
    ENDDO.
    IF lo_error IS BOUND.
      rv_text = rv_text && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
              && 'Further previous exceptions omitted (limit 8)'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD report_failure.
    " A recording sink lets the diagnostic tests exercise the real reporting
    " path without deliberately failing their own ABAP Unit result.
    IF mr_failure_log IS BOUND.
      APPEND i_text TO mr_failure_log->*.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail( quit = mv_assert_quit msg = i_text ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD decode_fixture.
    DATA(lv_content) = i_content.
    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture decoding / decode_fixture'.
    IF xstrlen( lv_content ) >= 3 AND lv_content(3) = mc_utf8_bom.
      lv_content = lv_content+3.
    ENDIF.
    TRY.
        DATA(lo_conv) = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>create(
          input = lv_content encoding = 'UTF-8' ignore_cerr = abap_false ).
        lo_conv->read( IMPORTING data = rv_json ).
      CATCH cx_sy_conversion_error cx_sy_codepage_converter_init INTO DATA(lx_utf8).
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason = |Helper=decode_fixture; bytes={ xstrlen( i_content ) }; |
                    && exception_text( lx_utf8 )
            previous = lx_utf8.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD setup.
    mv_assert_quit = if_aunit_constants=>method.
    CLEAR mv_group_test.
    reset_fixture( ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD reset_fixture.

    mo_cut = NEW #( ).
    CLEAR:
      mt_differences,
      mv_difference_count,
      mv_parity_reported,
      mv_fixture,
      mv_request,
      mv_phase.

    IF mv_group_test IS INITIAL.
      DATA lt_stack TYPE abap_callstack.
      DATA lv_reset_program TYPE sy-repid.
      CALL FUNCTION 'SYSTEM_CALLSTACK' IMPORTING callstack = lt_stack.
      LOOP AT lt_stack INTO DATA(ls_frame).
       IF lv_reset_program IS INITIAL.
         IF ls_frame-blocktype = 'METHOD' AND ls_frame-blockname = 'RESET_FIXTURE'.
           lv_reset_program = ls_frame-mainprogram.
         ENDIF.
         CONTINUE.
       ENDIF.
       IF ls_frame-blocktype = 'METHOD' AND ls_frame-mainprogram = lv_reset_program
          AND ls_frame-blockname <> 'SETUP'.
         mv_group_test = to_lower( ls_frame-blockname ).
       ENDIF.
       EXIT.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR mv_unused.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD run_scope_case.

    DATA ls_case TYPE ts_case.

    ls_case-fixture = |xsa-scope-{ i_number }.json|.
    ls_case-email   = |xsa-null-{ i_number }@takeda.com|.
    ls_case-flow    = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
    ls_case-pstype  = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.

    SELECT COUNT( * ) FROM zfi_das_roles
      WHERE user_emailid = @ls_case-email
      INTO @DATA(lv_role_rows).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lv_role_rows exp = 1
      msg = |Scope fixture requires one configured role for { ls_case-email }| ).
    IF lv_role_rows <> 1.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    run_paged_case( is_case = ls_case ).

    assert_no_differences( ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD run_paged_case.

    DATA:
      lv_expected_json   TYPE string,
      lv_actual_json     TYPE string,
      lt_expected_index  TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_actual_index    TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lv_skip            TYPE i,
      lv_page_rows       TYPE i,
      lv_total_count     TYPE int8,
      lv_finished        TYPE abap_bool.

    mv_fixture = is_case-fixture.
    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture decoding'.
    TRY.
    lv_expected_json =
      read_mime_fixture(
        i_fixture = is_case-fixture ).

    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture indexing / build_expected_index'.
    lt_expected_index =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = lv_expected_json ).

    IF i_complex_filter = abap_true OR i_cross_or_filter = abap_true.
      DATA lv_cross_or_witnesses TYPE i.
      LOOP AT lt_expected_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_candidate>).
        DATA(lt_properties) = read_object_properties(
          i_json        = lv_expected_json
          i_object_from = <ls_candidate>-object_from
          i_object_to   = <ls_candidate>-object_to ).
        DATA lv_matches TYPE abap_bool.
        IF i_complex_filter = abap_true.
          lv_matches = matches_complex_fixture( lt_properties ).
        ELSE.
          cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
            quit = mv_assert_quit
            msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (run_paged_case)|
            act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_properties[ name = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ] ) ) ).
          DATA(lv_supplier_token) = lt_properties[ name = 'SUPPLIERNAME' ]-token.
          DATA(lv_supplier) = unquote_json( lv_supplier_token ).
          lv_matches = xsdbool(
            <ls_candidate>-ponumber = '8000401022'
            OR find( val  = lv_supplier
                     sub  = 'Fuji'
                     case = abap_true ) >= 0 ).
          IF lv_matches = abap_true AND <ls_candidate>-ponumber <> '8000401022'.
            lv_cross_or_witnesses = lv_cross_or_witnesses + 1.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_matches = abap_false.
          DELETE TABLE lt_expected_index FROM <ls_candidate>.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      FREE lt_properties.
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = lt_expected_index
        msg  = 'The real fixture must contain positive complete-predicate witnesses' ).
      IF i_cross_or_filter = abap_true.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act  = xsdbool( lv_cross_or_witnesses > 0 )
          msg  = 'Cross-property OR must retain supplier witnesses outside the projected PO key' ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    WHILE lv_finished = abap_false.

      CLEAR:
        lv_actual_json,
        lv_total_count,
        lv_page_rows.

      mv_phase = 'SEGW request / fetch_gateway_page'.
      fetch_gateway_page(
        EXPORTING
          is_case       = is_case
          i_skip        = lv_skip
        IMPORTING
          e_json        = lv_actual_json
          e_total_count = lv_total_count ).

      mv_phase = 'SEGW page indexing / build_expected_index'.
      lt_actual_index =
        build_expected_index(
          i_json = lv_actual_json ).

      lv_page_rows = get_result_count( lv_actual_json ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = xsdbool( lv_page_rows <= mc_page_size )
        msg  = 'Fixture parity must retain at most one bounded Gateway page' ).

      mv_phase = 'Parity comparison / compare_actual_page'.
      compare_actual_page(
        EXPORTING
          i_fixture         = is_case-fixture
          i_expected_json   = lv_expected_json
          i_actual_json     = lv_actual_json
          it_actual_index   = lt_actual_index
        CHANGING
          ct_expected_index = lt_expected_index ).

      IF  lv_total_count <> lines( lt_expected_index ).
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Inline count differs; Fixture={ is_case-fixture }; |
            && |XSA={ lines( lt_expected_index ) }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_total_count }| ).
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_page_rows = 0.
        lv_finished = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

      lv_skip = lv_skip + lv_page_rows.

      IF lv_total_count > 0.
        IF lv_skip >= lv_total_count.
          lv_finished = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
      ELSEIF lv_page_rows < mc_page_size.
        lv_finished = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_skip > lines( lt_expected_index ) + mc_page_size.
        add_difference( |Paging exceeded the fixture cardinality; Fixture={ is_case-fixture }| ).
        lv_finished = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
      FREE: lt_actual_index, lv_actual_json.

    ENDWHILE.

    LOOP AT lt_expected_index
      ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>)
      WHERE matched = abap_false.

      add_difference(
        i_text =
            |Missing SEGW raw JSON row; Fixture={ is_case-fixture }; |
          && |PO={ <ls_expected>-ponumber }; |
          && |ITEMNO={ <ls_expected>-itemno }| ).

    ENDLOOP.

    FREE:
      lt_expected_index,
      lt_actual_index,
      lv_expected_json,
      lv_actual_json.
    CATCH zcx_fi_das_error cx_sy_conversion_error cx_sy_range_out_of_bounds
          cx_sy_open_sql_db INTO DATA(lx_case).
      DATA(lv_case_error) = exception_text( lx_case ).
      IF mv_difference_count > 0 AND mv_parity_reported = abap_false.
        lv_case_error = lv_case_error && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
                     && difference_text( ).
        mv_parity_reported = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = lv_case_error previous = lx_case.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD fetch_gateway_page.

    CLEAR:
      e_json,
      e_total_count.

    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING
        is_case        = is_case
        i_skip         = i_skip
      IMPORTING
        e_json         = e_json
        e_inline_count = e_total_count ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD get_gateway_response.

    DATA lv_path TYPE string.
    CLEAR: e_json, e_inline_count.

    lv_path =
      build_resource_path(
        is_case = is_case
        i_skip  = i_skip ).
    mv_fixture = is_case-fixture.
    mv_request = bounded_text( i_text = lv_path i_limit = 512 ).
    mv_phase = 'SEGW request / get_gateway_response'.

    TRY.
    e_json =
      execute_gateway_get(
        i_resource_path = lv_path ).

    IF is_case-inlinecount = abap_true
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.

      mv_phase = 'SEGW inline-count parsing / read_inline_count'.
      e_inline_count =
        read_inline_count(
          i_json = e_json ).

    ENDIF.
    CATCH zcx_fi_das_error cx_sy_conversion_error cx_sy_range_out_of_bounds
          INTO DATA(lx_gateway).
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = exception_text( lx_gateway ) previous = lx_gateway.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD build_resource_path.

    DATA:
      lv_email   TYPE string,
      lv_poowner TYPE string,
      lv_pstype  TYPE string,
      lv_top     TYPE i,
      lv_orderby TYPE string,
      lv_orderby_upper TYPE string,
      lv_orderby_direction TYPE string.

    lv_email = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-email ).
    lv_poowner = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-poowner ).
    lv_pstype = odata_quote( i_value = is_case-pstype ).
    lv_top = mc_page_size.

    IF is_case-top_specified = abap_true.
      lv_top = is_case-top.
    ELSEIF is_case-top > 0.
      lv_top = is_case-top.
    ENDIF.

    CASE is_case-flow.
      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |openPOParameters(IP_PSTYPE='{ lv_pstype }',|
          && |IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.

      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |thresholdParameters(IP_PSTYPE='{ lv_pstype }',|
          && |IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.

      WHEN zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
        rv_path =
            '/sap/opu/odata/sap/ZFI_DAS_SRV/'
          && |exclusionPOParameters(IP_EMAIL='{ lv_email }',|
          && |IP_POOWNER='{ lv_poowner }')/Results|.
    ENDCASE.

    rv_path =
        rv_path
      && |?$format=json|.

    IF is_case-inlinecount = abap_true
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$inlinecount=allpages|.
    ENDIF.

    IF ( is_case-top_specified = abap_true AND is_case-top > 0 )
       OR ( is_case-top_specified = abap_true
            AND is_case-top = 0
            AND is_case-fixture IS INITIAL )
       OR is_case-fixture IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$top={ lv_top }|.
    ENDIF.

    rv_path = rv_path && |&$skip={ i_skip }|.

    IF is_case-orderby IS NOT INITIAL.

      lv_orderby = trim_text( is_case-orderby ).
      lv_orderby_upper = to_upper( trim_text( lv_orderby ) ).

      IF is_case-orderby_desc = abap_true
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* DESC'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* DESCENDING'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* ASC'
         AND lv_orderby_upper NP '* ASCENDING'.
        lv_orderby_direction = ` desc`.
      ENDIF.

      lv_orderby = lv_orderby && lv_orderby_direction.

      cl_http_utility=>escape_url(
        EXPORTING
          unescaped = lv_orderby
        RECEIVING
          escaped   = lv_orderby ).

      rv_path = rv_path && |&$orderby={ lv_orderby }|.

    ENDIF.

    IF is_case-select_list IS NOT INITIAL.
      rv_path = rv_path && |&$select={ is_case-select_list }|.
    ENDIF.

    append_filter(
      EXPORTING
        is_case = is_case
      CHANGING
        cv_path = rv_path ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD append_filter.
    DATA: lt_properties TYPE tt_property_name,
          lt_clauses TYPE stringtab,
          lt_include TYPE stringtab,
          lt_exclude TYPE stringtab,
          lv_part TYPE string,
          lv_group TYPE string.
    LOOP AT is_case-filter_select_options INTO DATA(ls_filter).
      INSERT VALUE #( name = ls_filter-property ) INTO TABLE lt_properties.
    ENDLOOP.
    LOOP AT lt_properties INTO DATA(ls_property).
      CLEAR: lt_include, lt_exclude.
      LOOP AT is_case-filter_select_options INTO ls_filter
        WHERE property = ls_property-name.
        DATA(lv_low) = odata_quote( ls_filter-low ).
        DATA(lv_high) = odata_quote( ls_filter-high ).
        CASE ls_filter-option.
          WHEN 'EQ'.
            lv_part = |{ ls_property-name } eq '{ lv_low }'|.
          WHEN 'NE'.
            lv_part = |{ ls_property-name } ne '{ lv_low }'|.
          WHEN 'BT'.
            lv_part = |({ ls_property-name } ge '{ lv_low }' and |
                   && |{ ls_property-name } le '{ lv_high }')|.
          WHEN 'CP'.
            " Gateway CP contains patterns have only outer asterisks.
            DATA(lv_cp_length) = strlen( ls_filter-low ).
            IF lv_cp_length < 2.
              cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
                quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Fixture CP requires two outer asterisks' ).
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
            DATA(lv_cp_last) = lv_cp_length - 1.
            IF ls_filter-low(1) <> '*' OR ls_filter-low+lv_cp_last(1) <> '*'.
              cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
                quit = mv_assert_quit msg = 'Fixture CP requires two outer asterisks' ).
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
            lv_low = odata_quote( substring(
              val = ls_filter-low off = 1 len = lv_cp_length - 2 ) ).
            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_low CS '*' OR lv_low CS '+' OR lv_low CS '#' )
              msg  = 'This fixture URI helper supports only substring CP patterns' ).
            IF lv_low CS '*' OR lv_low CS '+' OR lv_low CS '#'.
              RETURN.
            ENDIF.
            lv_part = |substringof('{ lv_low }',{ ls_property-name })|.
          WHEN OTHERS.
            cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              msg  = |Unsupported fixture range option { ls_filter-option }| ).
            RETURN.
        ENDCASE.
        IF ls_filter-sign = 'E'.
          APPEND |not ({ lv_part })| TO lt_exclude.
        ELSE.
          APPEND lv_part TO lt_include.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF lt_include IS NOT INITIAL.
        CONCATENATE LINES OF lt_include INTO lv_group SEPARATED BY ' or '.
        APPEND |({ lv_group })| TO lt_clauses.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND LINES OF lt_exclude TO lt_clauses.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF is_case-filter_raw IS NOT INITIAL.
      APPEND |({ is_case-filter_raw })| TO lt_clauses.
    ENDIF.
    IF lt_clauses IS NOT INITIAL.
      CONCATENATE LINES OF lt_clauses INTO lv_group SEPARATED BY ' and '.
      cv_path = cv_path && |&$filter={ cl_http_utility=>escape_url( lv_group ) }|.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD odata_quote.

    rv_value = CONV string( i_value ).
    SHIFT rv_value RIGHT DELETING TRAILING space.

    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF ''''
      IN rv_value
      WITH ''''''.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD execute_gateway_get.

    DATA:
      lv_status_code TYPE i,
      lv_status_text TYPE string.

    execute_gateway_raw(
      EXPORTING
        i_resource_path = i_resource_path
      IMPORTING
        e_status_code   = lv_status_code
        e_status_text   = lv_status_text
        e_json          = rv_json ).

    IF lv_status_code < 200 OR lv_status_code >= 300.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason =
              |SEGW returned HTTP { lv_status_code } { lv_status_text }; |
            && |URI={ bounded_text( i_text = i_resource_path i_limit = 512 ) }; |
            && |Body length={ strlen( rv_json ) }; inspect Gateway error log (no payload logged)|.
    ENDIF.

    FIND REGEX '"original_row_index"[[:space:]]*:' IN rv_json IGNORING CASE.
    DATA(lv_find_subrc) = sy-subrc.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_find_subrc
      exp  = 4
      msg  = 'The internal original_row_index must never be serialized by Gateway' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD execute_gateway_raw.

    DATA:
      lo_client          TYPE REF TO /iwfnd/cl_sutil_client_proxy,
      lt_request_header  TYPE /iwfnd/sutil_property_t,
      lt_response_header TYPE /iwfnd/sutil_property_t,
      lv_response_body   TYPE xstring.

    lt_request_header = VALUE #(
      ( name  = '~request_method'
        value = 'GET' )
      ( name  = '~request_uri'
        value = i_resource_path )
      ( name  = 'accept'
        value = 'application/json' ) ).

    lo_client =
      /iwfnd/cl_sutil_client_proxy=>get_instance( ).

    lo_client->web_request(
      EXPORTING
        it_request_header  = lt_request_header
      IMPORTING
        ev_status_code     = e_status_code
        ev_status_text     = e_status_text
        et_response_header = lt_response_header
        ev_response_body   = lv_response_body ).

    e_json =
      /iwfnd/cl_sutil_xml_helper=>transform_to_string(
        lv_response_body ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_inline_count.

    DATA:
      lv_start  TYPE i,
      lv_end    TYPE i,
      lv_token  TYPE string.

    lv_start = find_odata_property( i_json = i_json i_property = '__count' ).

    lv_end = read_json_value_end(
      i_text  = i_json
      i_start = lv_start ).

    lv_token = substring(
      val = i_json
      off = lv_start
      len = lv_end - lv_start ).

    lv_token = unquote_json(
      i_token = lv_token ).

    IF lv_token IS INITIAL OR lv_token CN '0123456789'.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'Inline count must be a nonnegative integer'.
    ENDIF.
    rv_count = CONV int8( lv_token ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD get_result_count.

    DATA:
      lv_array_from TYPE i,
      lv_array_to   TYPE i,
      lv_position   TYPE i,
      lv_value_end  TYPE i,
      lv_char       TYPE char1.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json       = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position = lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
      IF lv_char <> '{'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData results must contain objects'.
      ENDIF.

      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_position ).

      rv_count = rv_count + 1.
      lv_position = lv_value_end.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_inlinecount_absent.

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = xsdbool( i_json CS '"__count"' )
      msg  = 'Response must not contain __count when $inlinecount was not requested' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_all_rows_match_filter.

    DATA:
      lv_row_count   TYPE i,
      lv_row_index   TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i,
      lv_actual      TYPE string,
      lv_expected    TYPE string.

    lv_row_count =
      get_result_count( i_json ).

    lv_expected = i_value.

    WHILE lv_row_index < lv_row_count.

      lv_row_index = lv_row_index + 1.

      find_result_object_bounds(
        EXPORTING
          i_json        = i_json
          i_index       = lv_row_index
        IMPORTING
          e_object_from = lv_object_from
          e_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

      DATA(lt_properties) =
        read_object_properties(
          i_json        = i_json
          i_object_from = lv_object_from
          i_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

      READ TABLE lt_properties ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_property>)
        WITH TABLE KEY name = i_property.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  =
            |Response row { lv_row_index } does not contain property { i_property }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      lv_actual = unquote_json( i_token = <ls_property>-token ).

      IF i_contains = abap_true.

        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act  = xsdbool( find( val = lv_actual sub = lv_expected
                               case = abap_true ) >= 0 )
          msg  =
            |Row { lv_row_index } property { i_property } must contain { i_value }| ).

      ELSE.

        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act  = lv_actual
          exp  = lv_expected
          msg  =
            |Row { lv_row_index } property { i_property } must equal { i_value }| ).

      ENDIF.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_fixture_window.
    " Independent fixture selection for these simple, explicitly supplied
    " predicates, not a reconstruction from Gateway select-options or SQL.
    DATA lt_fixtures TYPE tt_string.
    DATA lt_expected TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_key WITH EMPTY KEY.
    IF is_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_exclude.
      lt_fixtures = VALUE #( ( `xsa-exclude.json` ) ).
    ELSE.
      DATA(lv_flow) = COND string(
        WHEN is_case-flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_under THEN 'under' ELSE 'over' ).
      IF is_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all
         OR is_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_material.
        APPEND |xsa-{ lv_flow }-material.json| TO lt_fixtures.
      ENDIF.
      IF is_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_all
         OR is_case-pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service.
        APPEND |xsa-{ lv_flow }-service.json| TO lt_fixtures.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    LOOP AT lt_fixtures INTO DATA(lv_fixture).
      DATA(lv_fixture_json) = read_mime_fixture( lv_fixture ).
      mv_phase = 'EXPECTED filter fixture indexing / assert_fixture_window'.
      DATA(lt_index) = build_expected_index( lv_fixture_json ).
      LOOP AT lt_index INTO DATA(ls_index).
        DATA(lt_properties) = read_object_properties(
          i_json = lv_fixture_json i_object_from = ls_index-object_from i_object_to = ls_index-object_to ).
        IF i_property IS NOT INITIAL.
          IF NOT line_exists( lt_properties[ name = i_property ] ).
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = |Fixture={ lv_fixture }; Helper=assert_fixture_window; missing { i_property }|.
          ENDIF.
          DATA(lv_token) = lt_properties[ name = i_property ]-token.
          IF lv_token = 'null'.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
          DATA(lv_value) = unquote_json( lv_token ).
          IF i_contains = abap_true.
            IF find( val = lv_value sub = i_value case = abap_true ) < 0.
              CONTINUE.
            ENDIF.
          ELSEIF lv_value <> i_value
                 AND ( i_alternative IS INITIAL OR lv_value <> i_alternative ).
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        IF i_property_2 IS NOT INITIAL.
          IF NOT line_exists( lt_properties[ name = i_property_2 ] ).
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = |Fixture={ lv_fixture }; Helper=assert_fixture_window; missing { i_property_2 }|.
          ENDIF.
          lv_token = lt_properties[ name = i_property_2 ]-token.
          IF lv_token = 'null'.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
          lv_value = unquote_json( lv_token ).
          IF i_contains_2 = abap_true.
            IF find( val = lv_value sub = i_value_2 case = abap_true ) < 0.
              CONTINUE.
            ENDIF.
          ELSEIF lv_value <> i_value_2.
            CONTINUE.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        DATA(ls_key) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_key(
          ponumber = ls_index-ponumber itemno = ls_index-itemno ).
        IF NOT line_exists( lt_properties[ name = 'PSTYPE' ] ).
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = |Fixture={ lv_fixture }; Helper=assert_fixture_window; missing PSTYPE|.
        ENDIF.
        lv_token = lt_properties[ name = 'PSTYPE' ]-token.
        ls_key-pstype = unquote_json( lv_token ).
        APPEND ls_key TO lt_expected.
      ENDLOOP.
      FREE: lv_fixture_json, lt_index, lt_properties.
    ENDLOOP.
    SORT lt_expected BY pstype DESCENDING ponumber itemno.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = i_count exp = lines( lt_expected )
      msg = |{ mv_group_test }: Total membership for predicate "{ is_case-filter_raw }" from { lt_fixtures[ 1 ] }| ).
    mv_phase = 'SEGW fixture-window membership / assert_fixture_window'.
    DATA(lv_page_size) = lines( lt_expected ).
    IF is_case-top_specified = abap_true AND is_case-top < lv_page_size.
      lv_page_size = is_case-top.
    ENDIF.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = get_result_count( i_json ) exp = lv_page_size
      msg = |{ mv_group_test }: Filtered page must contain the entire expected key window| ).
    DO lv_page_size TIMES.
      DATA(lv_index) = sy-index.
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act = read_result_property( i_json = i_json i_index = lv_index i_property = 'PONUMBER' )
        exp = CONV string( lt_expected[ lv_index ]-ponumber )
        msg = |{ mv_group_test }; row={ lv_index }; Field=PONUMBER; exact fixture membership/order| ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act = read_result_property( i_json = i_json i_index = lv_index i_property = 'ITEMNO' )
        exp = CONV string( lt_expected[ lv_index ]-itemno )
        msg = |{ mv_group_test }; row={ lv_index }; Field=ITEMNO; exact fixture membership/order| ).
      IF is_case-select_list IS INITIAL OR is_case-select_list CS 'PSTYPE'.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          act = read_result_property( i_json = i_json i_index = lv_index i_property = 'PSTYPE' )
          exp = CONV string( lt_expected[ lv_index ]-pstype )
          msg = |{ mv_group_test }; row={ lv_index }; Field=PSTYPE; exact fixture value| ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDDO.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_rows_sorted.

    DATA:
    lv_row_count  TYPE i,
    lv_row_index  TYPE i,
    lv_previous   TYPE string,
    lv_current    TYPE string,
    lv_previous_i TYPE i,
    lv_current_i  TYPE i,
    lv_previous_n TYPE decfloat34,
    lv_current_n  TYPE decfloat34.

    IF i_property <> 'PSTYPE'
     AND i_property <> 'SUPPLIERNAME'
     AND i_property <> 'AMOUNTACCRUED'
     AND i_property <> 'REVIWED_BY'.

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |assert_rows_sorted does not support { i_property }| ).

    ENDIF.

    lv_row_count =
    get_result_count( i_json ).

    WHILE lv_row_index < lv_row_count.

      lv_row_index = lv_row_index + 1.

      find_result_object_bounds(
        EXPORTING i_json = i_json i_index = lv_row_index
        IMPORTING e_object_from = DATA(lv_from) e_object_to = DATA(lv_to) ).
      DATA(lt_properties) = read_object_properties(
        i_json = i_json i_object_from = lv_from i_object_to = lv_to ).
      IF NOT line_exists( lt_properties[ name = i_property ] ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>fail(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg = |Sorted response row { lv_row_index } omits { i_property }| ).
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      DATA(lv_token) = lt_properties[ name = i_property ]-token.
      lv_current = COND #( WHEN lv_token = 'null' THEN ``
                          ELSE unquote_json( lv_token ) ).

      IF lv_row_index > 1.

        IF i_property = 'PSTYPE'.

          lv_previous_i =
          COND i(
            WHEN lv_previous IS INITIAL
            THEN 0
            ELSE CONV i( lv_previous ) ).

          lv_current_i =
          COND i(
            WHEN lv_current IS INITIAL
            THEN 0
            ELSE CONV i( lv_current ) ).

          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous_i >= lv_current_i )
              msg  =
              |PSTYPE descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous_i <= lv_current_i )
              msg  =
              |PSTYPE ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ELSEIF i_property = 'AMOUNTACCRUED'.

          lv_previous_n =
          COND decfloat34(
            WHEN lv_previous IS INITIAL
            THEN 0
            ELSE CONV decfloat34( lv_previous ) ).

          lv_current_n =
          COND decfloat34(
            WHEN lv_current IS INITIAL
            THEN 0
            ELSE CONV decfloat34( lv_current ) ).

          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous_n >= lv_current_n )
              msg  =
              |AMOUNTACCRUED descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous_n <= lv_current_n )
              msg  =
              |AMOUNTACCRUED ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ELSE.

        " Do not uppercase: production ABAP SORT uses the original
        " character values. The assertion must use the same values.
          IF i_descending = abap_true.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous >= lv_current )
              msg  =
              |{ i_property } descending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ELSE.

            cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
              quit = mv_assert_quit
              act  = xsdbool( lv_previous <= lv_current )
              msg  =
              |{ i_property } ascending violation at row { lv_row_index }: |
              && |previous="{ lv_previous }", current="{ lv_current }"| ).

          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      lv_previous = lv_current.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_no_duplica_across_pages.

    DATA:
      lt_page_1 TYPE tt_expected_index,
      lt_page_2 TYPE tt_expected_index.

    lt_page_1 =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = i_json_page_1 ).

    lt_page_2 =
      build_expected_index(
        i_json = i_json_page_2 ).

    LOOP AT lt_page_2 ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_page_2>).

      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = xsdbool(
          line_exists(
            lt_page_1[
              ponumber = <ls_page_2>-ponumber
              itemno   = <ls_page_2>-itemno ] ) )
        msg  =
          |Page 2 must not repeat page 1 row { <ls_page_2>-ponumber }/{ <ls_page_2>-itemno }| ).

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_page_windows.
    DATA(ls_case) = is_case.
    DATA(lv_rows_1) = get_result_count( i_json_page_1 ).
    DATA(lv_rows_2) = get_result_count( i_json_page_2 ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = xsdbool( read_inline_count( i_json_page_1 ) > is_case-top )
      msg = 'Paging fixture must provide positive witnesses on both pages' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = read_inline_count( i_json_page_2 )
      exp = read_inline_count( i_json_page_1 )
      msg = 'The total count must not change with page offset' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act = lv_rows_1 exp = COND i(
        WHEN read_inline_count( i_json_page_1 ) < is_case-top
        THEN CONV i( read_inline_count( i_json_page_1 ) ) ELSE is_case-top )
      msg = 'The first page must fill the requested window unless the result ends' ).
    ls_case-top = is_case-top * 2.
    ls_case-top_specified = abap_true.
    get_gateway_response(
      EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
      IMPORTING e_json = DATA(lv_prefix) e_inline_count = DATA(lv_count) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = get_result_count( lv_prefix )
      exp = lv_rows_1 + lv_rows_2
      msg = 'Two adjacent pages must cover the complete combined prefix' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lv_count exp = read_inline_count( i_json_page_1 ) ).
    DATA(lt_prefix_keys) = build_expected_index( lv_prefix ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit act = lines( lt_prefix_keys ) exp = lv_rows_1 + lv_rows_2 ).
    DO lv_rows_1 TIMES.
      assert_row_keys_match(
        i_json_left = i_json_page_1 i_left_index = sy-index
        i_json_right = lv_prefix i_right_index = sy-index ).
    ENDDO.
    DO lv_rows_2 TIMES.
      DATA(lv_index) = sy-index.
      assert_row_keys_match(
        i_json_left = i_json_page_2 i_left_index = lv_index
        i_json_right = lv_prefix i_right_index = lv_rows_1 + lv_index ).
    ENDDO.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_row_keys_match.

    DATA:
      lv_left_from   TYPE i,
      lv_left_to     TYPE i,
      lv_right_from  TYPE i,
      lv_right_to    TYPE i,
      lv_left_po     TYPE string,
      lv_left_item   TYPE string,
      lv_right_po    TYPE string,
      lv_right_item  TYPE string,
      lv_match       TYPE abap_bool.

    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_left
        i_index       = i_left_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = lv_left_from
        e_object_to   = lv_left_to ).

    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_right
        i_index       = i_right_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = lv_right_from
        e_object_to   = lv_right_to ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_left
        i_object_from = lv_left_from
        i_object_to   = lv_left_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = lv_left_po
        e_itemno      = lv_left_item ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json_right
        i_object_from = lv_right_from
        i_object_to   = lv_right_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = lv_right_po
        e_itemno      = lv_right_item ).

    lv_match =
      xsdbool(
        lv_left_po = lv_right_po
        AND lv_left_item = lv_right_item ).

    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_match
      exp  = i_expected_match
      msg  =
        |Unexpected row-key match result between row { i_left_index } and row { i_right_index }| ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_result_object_bounds.

    DATA:
      lv_array_from TYPE i,
      lv_array_to   TYPE i,
      lv_position   TYPE i,
      lv_found      TYPE i,
      lv_char       TYPE char1,
      lv_value_end  TYPE i.

    IF i_index <= 0.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason = 'Result row index must be greater than zero'.
    ENDIF.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json       = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position = lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      lv_found = lv_found + 1.
      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_position ).

      IF lv_found = i_index.
        IF lv_char <> '{'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING
              i_reason =
                |Result row { i_index } is not a JSON object|.
        ENDIF.

        e_object_from = lv_position.
        e_object_to = lv_value_end - 1.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.

      lv_position = lv_value_end.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      EXPORTING
        i_reason =
          |Result row { i_index } was not found in the Gateway response|.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_mime_fixture.

    DATA:
      lo_mime    TYPE REF TO if_mr_api,
      lv_content TYPE xstring.
    mv_fixture = i_fixture.
    mv_phase = 'EXPECTED fixture MIME read / read_mime_fixture'.

    lo_mime =
      cl_mime_repository_api=>get_api( ).

    lo_mime->get(
      EXPORTING
        i_url              = mc_mime_root && i_fixture
      IMPORTING
        e_content          = lv_content
      EXCEPTIONS
        parameter_missing  = 1
        error_occured      = 2
        not_found          = 3
        permission_failure = 4
        OTHERS             = 5 ).

    DATA(lv_mime_subrc) = sy-subrc.
    IF lv_mime_subrc <> 0
       OR lv_content IS INITIAL.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason =
                     |Test={ mv_group_test }; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
                  && |Phase={ mv_phase }; MIME subrc={ lv_mime_subrc }; |
                  && |bytes={ xstrlen( lv_content ) }; Path={ mc_mime_root }{ i_fixture }|.

    ENDIF.

    rv_json = decode_fixture( lv_content ).

    FREE lv_content.

    CLEAR:
      lo_mime.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD build_expected_index.

    DATA:
      lv_array_from  TYPE i,
      lv_array_to    TYPE i,
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i,
      lv_ponumber    TYPE string,
      lv_itemno      TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1,
      ls_expected    TYPE ts_expected_index.

    CLEAR rt_index.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json       = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_position =
      lv_array_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_array_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= lv_array_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char =
        i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.

        lv_position =
          lv_position + 1.

        CONTINUE.

      ENDIF.

      IF lv_char <> '{'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       |Unexpected character in results array at offset { lv_position }|.

      ENDIF.

      lv_object_from =
        lv_position.

      lv_object_to =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_object_from
          i_open  = '{'
          i_close = '}' ).

      IF lv_object_to > lv_array_to.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       'JSON result object exceeds the results array'.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:
        lv_ponumber,
        lv_itemno.

      read_json_row_key(
        EXPORTING
          i_json        = i_json
          i_object_from = lv_object_from
          i_object_to   = lv_object_to
        IMPORTING
          e_ponumber    = lv_ponumber
          e_itemno      = lv_itemno ).

      IF lv_ponumber IS INITIAL
         OR lv_itemno IS INITIAL.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       'JSON row does not contain PONUMBER and ITEMNO'.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR ls_expected.

      ls_expected-ponumber =
        lv_ponumber.

      ls_expected-itemno =
        lv_itemno.

      ls_expected-object_from =
        lv_object_from.

      ls_expected-object_to =
        lv_object_to.

      INSERT ls_expected
        INTO TABLE rt_index.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason =
              |Duplicate JSON key; PO={ lv_ponumber }; |
            && |ITEMNO={ lv_itemno }|.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_object_to + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_results_array_bounds.

    e_array_from = find_odata_property( i_json = i_json i_property = 'results' ).
    IF i_json+e_array_from(1) <> '['.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason =
                     'Fixture results array start was not found'.

    ENDIF.

    e_array_to = read_json_value_end( i_text = i_json i_start = e_array_from ) - 1.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_odata_property.
    DATA(lv_length) = strlen( i_json ).
    DATA(lv_position) = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = 0 ).
    DATA lv_depth TYPE i.
    DATA lv_closed TYPE abap_bool.
    DATA lt_root_keys TYPE tt_json_property.
    DATA lt_data_keys TYPE tt_json_property.
    TRY.
    " Accept one leading Unicode BOM without shifting any stored row offsets.
    DATA(lv_bom) = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>uccpi( 65279 ).
    IF lv_length > 0 AND i_json(1) = lv_bom(1).
      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = 1 ).
    ENDIF.
    rv_position = -1.
    IF lv_position >= lv_length OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> '{'.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData response must be a JSON object'.
    ENDIF.
    lv_position = lv_position + 1.
    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.
      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position ).
      IF lv_position >= lv_length.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
      IF i_json+lv_position(1) = '}'.
        lv_position = lv_position + 1.
        IF lv_depth = 0.
          lv_closed = abap_true.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
        lv_depth = 0.
      ELSE.
        IF i_json+lv_position(1) <> '"'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData envelope property must be a JSON string'.
        ENDIF.
        DATA(lv_key_end) = read_json_string_end(
          i_text = i_json i_start = lv_position ) + 1.
        DATA(lv_name) = unquote_json( substring(
          val = i_json off = lv_position len = lv_key_end - lv_position ) ).
        IF lv_depth = 0.
          INSERT VALUE #( name = lv_name ) INTO TABLE lt_root_keys.
        ELSE.
          INSERT VALUE #( name = lv_name ) INTO TABLE lt_data_keys.
        ENDIF.
        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = |Duplicate OData envelope property { lv_name }|.
        ENDIF.
        lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_key_end ).
        IF lv_position >= lv_length OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ':'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData envelope property has no colon'.
        ENDIF.
        lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position + 1 ).
        IF lv_position >= lv_length.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_depth = 0 AND lv_name = 'error'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'Gateway returned an OData error envelope'.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_depth = 0 AND lv_name = 'd'.
          IF i_json+lv_position(1) <> '{'.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData d envelope must be an object'.
          ENDIF.
          lv_depth = 1.
          lv_position = lv_position + 1.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_depth = 1 AND lv_name = i_property.
          rv_position = lv_position.
        ENDIF.
        lv_position = read_json_value_end( i_text = i_json i_start = lv_position ).
      ENDIF.
      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position ).
      IF lv_position >= lv_length.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
      IF i_json+lv_position(1) = ','.
        lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position + 1 ).
        IF lv_position >= lv_length OR i_json+lv_position(1) = '}'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'Trailing OData envelope comma'.
        ENDIF.
      ELSEIF i_json+lv_position(1) <> '}'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'OData envelope properties require a comma'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDWHILE.
    lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position ).
    IF lv_closed = abap_false OR lv_position <> lv_length OR rv_position < 0.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = |Missing/invalid OData d/{ i_property } envelope|.
    ENDIF.
    CATCH zcx_fi_das_error cx_sy_range_out_of_bounds INTO DATA(lx_envelope).
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason = |Helper=find_odata_property; Property={ i_property }; |
                  && |offset={ lv_position }; length={ lv_length }; |
                  && exception_text( lx_envelope )
          previous = lx_envelope.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_row_key.

    DATA:
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_key_start   TYPE i,
      lv_key_end     TYPE i,
      lv_value_start TYPE i,
      lv_value_end   TYPE i,
      lv_key         TYPE string,
      lv_token       TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1.

    CLEAR:
      e_ponumber,
      e_itemno.

    lv_position =
      i_object_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < i_object_to.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char =
        i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','.

        lv_position =
          lv_position + 1.

        CONTINUE.

      ENDIF.

      IF lv_char <> '"'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       'Invalid JSON object property name'.

      ENDIF.

      lv_key_start =
        lv_position.

      lv_key_end =
        read_json_string_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_key_start ).

      lv_key =
        unquote_json(
          i_token =
            substring(
              val = i_json
              off = lv_key_start
              len = lv_key_end - lv_key_start + 1 ) ).

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_key_end + 1 ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to
         OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ':'.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       |JSON property { lv_key } has no colon|.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        skip_whitespace(
          i_text     = i_json
          i_position = lv_position + 1 ).

      lv_value_start =
        lv_position.

      lv_value_end =
        read_json_value_end(
          i_text  = i_json
          i_start = lv_value_start ).

      IF lv_value_end > i_object_to + 1.

        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason =
                       |JSON property { lv_key } exceeds its object boundary|.

      ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Only create a value substring for the two key properties.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      IF lv_key = 'PONUMBER'
         OR lv_key = 'ITEMNO'.

        lv_token =
          substring(
            val = i_json
            off = lv_value_start
            len = lv_value_end - lv_value_start ).
        IF strlen( lv_token ) < 2 OR lv_token(1) <> '"'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = |JSON key { lv_key } must be a string|.
        ENDIF.

        IF lv_key = 'PONUMBER'.

          e_ponumber =
            unquote_json(
              i_token = lv_token ).

        ELSE.

          e_itemno =
            unquote_json(
              i_token = lv_token ).

        ENDIF.

        FREE lv_token.

        IF e_ponumber IS NOT INITIAL
