           AND e_itemno IS NOT INITIAL.

          RETURN.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      CLEAR lv_key.

      lv_position =
        lv_value_end + 1.

    ENDWHILE.
    IF e_ponumber IS INITIAL OR e_itemno IS INITIAL.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'JSON row requires nonempty PO/item keys'.
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_first_result_key.

    DATA:
      lv_array_from  TYPE i,
      lv_array_to    TYPE i,
      lv_object_from TYPE i,
      lv_object_to   TYPE i.

    find_results_array_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json       = i_json
      IMPORTING
        e_array_from = lv_array_from
        e_array_to   = lv_array_to ).

    lv_object_from =
      skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_array_from + 1 ).

    IF lv_object_from >= lv_array_to
       OR i_json+lv_object_from(1) <> '{'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_object_to =
      find_matching_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_object_from
        i_open  = '{'
        i_close = '}' ).

    read_json_row_key(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json
        i_object_from = lv_object_from
        i_object_to   = lv_object_to
      IMPORTING
        e_ponumber    = e_ponumber
        e_itemno      = e_itemno ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_first_result_property.

    rv_token =
      read_result_property(
        i_json     = i_json
        i_index    = 1
        i_property = i_property ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_result_property.

    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING
        i_json        = i_json
        i_index       = i_index
      IMPORTING
        e_object_from = DATA(lv_object_from)
        e_object_to   = DATA(lv_object_to) ).

    DATA(lt_properties) =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_json
        i_object_from = lv_object_from
        i_object_to   = lv_object_to ).

    READ TABLE lt_properties ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_property>)
      WITH TABLE KEY name = i_property.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      rv_token = <ls_property>-token.
    ENDIF.

    rv_token =
      unquote_json(
        i_token = rv_token ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_actual_page.

    LOOP AT it_actual_index ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_actual>).

      READ TABLE ct_expected_index
        ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>)
        WITH TABLE KEY
          ponumber = <ls_actual>-ponumber
          itemno   = <ls_actual>-itemno.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Extra SEGW raw JSON row; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ <ls_actual>-ponumber }; ITEMNO={ <ls_actual>-itemno }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      IF <ls_expected>-matched = abap_true.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Duplicate SEGW raw JSON key across pages; |
            && |Fixture={ i_fixture }; PO={ <ls_actual>-ponumber }; |
            && |ITEMNO={ <ls_actual>-itemno }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      compare_raw_object(
        i_fixture       = i_fixture
        i_ponumber      = <ls_actual>-ponumber
        i_itemno        = <ls_actual>-itemno
        i_expected_json = i_expected_json
        i_expected_from = <ls_expected>-object_from
        i_expected_to   = <ls_expected>-object_to
        i_actual_json   = i_actual_json
        i_actual_from   = <ls_actual>-object_from
        i_actual_to     = <ls_actual>-object_to ).

      <ls_expected>-matched = abap_true.

    ENDLOOP.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_raw_object.

    DATA:
      lt_expected TYPE tt_json_property,
      lt_actual   TYPE tt_json_property.

    lt_expected =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_expected_json
        i_object_from = i_expected_from
        i_object_to   = i_expected_to ).

    lt_actual =
      read_object_properties(
        i_json        = i_actual_json
        i_object_from = i_actual_from
        i_object_to   = i_actual_to ).

    LOOP AT lt_expected ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_expected>).

      READ TABLE lt_actual
        ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<ls_actual>)
        WITH TABLE KEY name = <ls_expected>-name.

      IF sy-subrc <> 0.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* XSA exposes a synthetic ID property that is not part of the migrated
* SEGW result contract and has no active UI consumer. Only this specific
* missing property is accepted. Every other missing property remains a
* parity failure.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
        IF ignore_missing_property(
             i_name = <ls_expected>-name ) = abap_true.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.

        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Missing SEGW raw JSON property; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ <ls_expected>-name }| ).
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      compare_raw_property(
        i_fixture        = i_fixture
        i_ponumber       = i_ponumber
        i_itemno         = i_itemno
        i_name           = <ls_expected>-name
        i_expected_token = <ls_expected>-token
        i_actual_token   = <ls_actual>-token ).

    ENDLOOP.

    LOOP AT lt_actual ASSIGNING <ls_actual>.
      IF NOT line_exists( lt_expected[ name = <ls_actual>-name ] ).
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Extra SEGW raw JSON property; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ <ls_actual>-name }| ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.

    FREE:
      lt_expected,
      lt_actual.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_object_properties.

    DATA:
      lv_position    TYPE i,
      lv_key_start   TYPE i,
      lv_key_end     TYPE i,
      lv_value_start TYPE i,
      lv_value_end   TYPE i,
      lv_key         TYPE string,
      lv_token       TYPE string,
      lv_char        TYPE char1.

    IF i_object_from < 0 OR i_object_to >= strlen( i_json )
       OR i_object_from >= i_object_to.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON object bounds'.
    ENDIF.
    IF i_json+i_object_from(1) <> '{' OR i_json+i_object_to(1) <> '}'.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON object delimiters'.
    ENDIF.
    lv_position = i_object_from + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < i_object_to.

      lv_position = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_position ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_char = i_json+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char <> '"'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason = |Invalid JSON property at offset { lv_position }|.
      ENDIF.

      lv_key_start = lv_position.
      lv_key_end = read_json_string_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_key_start ).

      lv_key = unquote_json(
        i_token = substring(
          val = i_json
          off = lv_key_start
          len = lv_key_end - lv_key_start + 1 ) ).

      lv_position = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_key_end + 1 ).

      IF lv_position >= i_object_to
         OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ':'.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason = |JSON property { lv_key } has no colon|.
      ENDIF.

      lv_value_start = skip_whitespace(
        i_text     = i_json
        i_position = lv_position + 1 ).

      lv_value_end = read_json_value_end(
        i_text  = i_json
        i_start = lv_value_start ).
      IF lv_value_end > i_object_to.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'JSON value exceeds its object'.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_key <> '__metadata'.
        lv_token = trim_text(
          i_text = substring(
            val = i_json
            off = lv_value_start
            len = lv_value_end - lv_value_start ) ).

        INSERT VALUE #(
          name  = lv_key
          token = lv_token
        ) INTO TABLE rt_properties.

        IF sy-subrc <> 0.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING
              i_reason = |Duplicate JSON property { lv_key }|.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      CLEAR:
        lv_key,
        lv_token.

      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_value_end ).
      IF lv_position = i_object_to.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
      IF lv_position > i_object_to OR i_json+lv_position(1) <> ','.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'JSON properties require a comma'.
      ENDIF.
      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_json i_position = lv_position + 1 ).
      IF lv_position >= i_object_to.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'Trailing JSON object comma'.
      ENDIF.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD compare_raw_property.

    DATA:
      lv_expected_token TYPE string,
      lv_actual_token   TYPE string,
      lv_expected_text  TYPE string,
      lv_actual_text    TYPE string,
      lv_expected_len   TYPE i,
      lv_actual_len     TYPE i.
    DATA(lv_expected_log) = bounded_text( i_text = i_expected_token i_limit = mc_max_token ).
    DATA(lv_actual_log) = bounded_text( i_text = i_actual_token i_limit = mc_max_token ).

    lv_expected_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_expected_token ).

    lv_actual_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_actual_token ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Documented functional compatibility for XSA SQL NULL values
*
* The compatibility contract allows specific initial ABAP values in
* place of XSA SQL NULL. Target Gateway serialization remains unexecuted.
* IS_EQUIVALENT_NULL contains a
* field-specific allowlist. There is intentionally no global rule such
* as NULL = empty string or NULL = zero.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF is_equivalent_null(
         i_name           = i_name
         i_expected_token = lv_expected_token
         i_actual_token   = lv_actual_token ) = abap_true.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Normalize non-NULL CHANGEDTIME values
*
* XSA can omit leading zeroes while SEGW emits two-digit components.
* Both representations describe the same Edm.Time value:
*
*   "PT9H40M11S" = "PT09H40M11S"
*   "PT8H24M4S"  = "PT08H24M04S"
*
* Only CHANGEDTIME enters this normalization. The actual hour, minute,
* and second values remain strict.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_name = 'CHANGEDTIME'.
      lv_expected_token =
        normalize_odata_time(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_token =
        normalize_odata_time(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_STRICT_001 - temporary aggregate-order exception
*
* Aggregate segment order can currently differ between XSA and SEGW.
* Both values are decoded first. Content and multiplicity must still be
* identical after ordering is ignored.
*
* Accepted:
*   XSA="A;B;C", SEGW="C;A;B"
*
* Rejected:
*   XSA="A;B;C", SEGW="A;B"
*   XSA="A;A;B", SEGW="A;B"
*
* Remove this branch after aggregate ordering is deterministic and
* aligned between both implementations.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF is_unordered_aggregate_field(
         i_name = i_name ) = abap_true
       AND strlen( lv_expected_token ) >= 2
       AND strlen( lv_actual_token ) >= 2
       AND lv_expected_token(1) = '"'
       AND lv_actual_token(1) = '"'.

      lv_expected_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).

      IF equal_aggregate_content(
           i_expected = lv_expected_text
           i_actual   = lv_actual_text ) = abap_false.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |Aggregate content differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_log }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_actual_log }| ).
      ENDIF.

      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Compare decoded JSON string values
*
* Different JSON tokens can represent exactly the same string received
* by the UI. UNQUOTE_JSON removes the outer quotes and decodes supported
* JSON escape sequences.
*
* Accepted examples:
*   XSA="S \u0026 K",              SEGW="S & K"
*   XSA="King\u0027s College",    SEGW="King's College"
*   XSA="\/Date(1677240711333)\/",
*   SEGW="/Date(1677240711333)/"
*
* Rejected examples:
*   XSA="King's College", SEGW="Kings College"
*   XSA="A & B",          SEGW="A and B"
*
* NULL, numbers, booleans, arrays, and objects do not enter this branch
* and remain subject to strict token comparison below.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lv_expected_len =
      strlen( lv_expected_token ).

    lv_actual_len =
      strlen( lv_actual_token ).

    IF lv_expected_len >= 2
       AND lv_actual_len >= 2
       AND lv_expected_token(1) = '"'
       AND lv_actual_token(1) = '"'.

      lv_expected_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_expected_token ).

      lv_actual_text =
        unquote_json(
          i_token = lv_actual_token ).

      IF lv_expected_text <> lv_actual_text.
        add_difference(
          i_text =
              |String value differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
            && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
            && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_log }; |
            && |SEGW={ lv_actual_log }| ).
      ENDIF.

      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Strict comparison for every remaining JSON token
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_expected_token <> lv_actual_token.
      add_difference(
        i_text =
            |Raw value differs; Fixture={ i_fixture }; |
          && |PO={ i_ponumber }; ITEMNO={ i_itemno }; |
          && |Field={ i_name }; XSA={ lv_expected_log }; |
          && |SEGW={ lv_actual_log }| ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD is_equivalent_null.

    rv_result = abap_false.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* TODO_PARITY_FUNCTIONAL_NULL_001
*
* Functional compatibility between XSA SQL NULL values and standard
* SEGW serialization of initial ABAP values.
*
* Background
* ----------
* XSA receives database NULL values directly from SAP HANA and emits:
*
*   null
*
* Classic SEGW receives typed ABAP values. Elementary ABAP value types
* do not preserve the distinction between an absent source value and an
* explicitly supplied initial value.
*
* The retained allowlist expects these initial-value representations;
* this is not evidence of execution on the target Gateway:
*
*   Character value  -> ""
*   Decimal value    -> "0.00"
*   TIMS value       -> "PT00H00M00S"
*
* Scope
* -----
* The rules below are deliberately field-specific. Do not replace them
* with global rules such as:
*
*   null = ""
*   null = "0.00"
*   null = "PT00H00M00S"
*
* NULL and an initial value can have different business meanings for
* other properties.
*
* The exceptions are also one-directional:
*
*   XSA null -> approved SEGW initial representation
*
* A non-NULL XSA value remains subject to strict comparison.
*
* The following examples are therefore NOT accepted:
*
*   XSA="0.00", SEGW=null
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="1.00"
*   XSA=null,   SEGW="X"
*
* Review this allowlist whenever UI consumption or the OData contract
* changes.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_expected_token <> 'null'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    CASE i_name.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Character properties serialized by SEGW as ""
*
* FULL_NAME
*   Explicit null-to-empty compatibility tolerance. This comparison
*   does not prove equivalence for every UI consumer.
*
* DECIMAL_FORMAT
*   This exception applies to DECIMAL_FORMAT in the open-PO result.
*   The effective user decimal format is loaded separately from the
*   userDetails service.
*
* DATFM and USERNAME
*   Explicit null-to-empty tolerances. Absence of active consumers has
*   not been established by the source audit.
*
* COSTCENTERDESC and MANAGEUNIT
*   Inspected XSA read functions project COSTCENTERDESC as literal NULL;
*   MANAGEUNIT is aggregated. Non-empty allowlisted values still use
*   unordered comparison, including duplicates.
*
* NOTIF_24_HR
*   The UI activates the notification indicator only when the value is
*   exactly "X". Both NULL and "" therefore leave it inactive.
*
* REVIWED_BY
*   The property name intentionally follows the existing OData contract.
*   Both XSA NULL and the SEGW empty string render as an empty reviewer.
*   The property remains present in the response, so the UI does not
*   receive an omitted or undefined property.
*
* Accepted examples:
*
*   FULL_NAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   DECIMAL_FORMAT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   DATFM:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   USERNAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   NOTIF_24_HR:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   REVIWED_BY:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   COSTCENTERDESC:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
*   MANAGEUNIT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW=""
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   FULL_NAME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="John Smith"
*
*   NOTIF_24_HR:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="X"
*
*   REVIWED_BY:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="USER123"
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'COSTCENTERDESC'
      OR 'DATFM'
      OR 'DECIMAL_FORMAT'
      OR 'FULL_NAME'
      OR 'MANAGEUNIT'
      OR 'NOTIF_24_HR'
      OR 'REVIWED_BY'
      OR 'USERNAME'.

        rv_result =
        xsdbool(
          i_actual_token = '""'
        ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Decimal properties serialized by SEGW as "0.00"
*
* EDGEWTDAMT
*   Business.controller.js and TBS.controller.js explicitly normalize
*   an XSA NULL value to the string "0.00" before passing the amount to
*   the accrual calculation:
*
*     rData.EDGEWTDAMT === null
*       ? "0.00"
*       : rData.EDGEWTDAMT
*
*   This establishes equivalence for those calculation inputs, not
*   every UI consumer or target Gateway serialization.
*
* GRVALUE_USD
*   The UI already normalizes an XSA NULL amount to 0.00 before currency
*   formatting. SEGW "0.00" therefore produces the same visible result.
*
* INVVALUE_USD
*   This converted/display amount is initial in SEGW when XSA has no
*   source value. The exception is intentionally limited to INVVALUE_USD.
*   INVVALUE remains strict because it participates in accrual arithmetic.
*
* INVVALUE_CAL
*   Explicit null-to-zero tolerance for this helper property; universal
*   UI equivalence has not been established. The exception remains
*   field-specific and must not be generalized to INVVALUE.
*
* LASTMONTHWRKCOMP
*   XSA NULL displays as an empty value. SEGW "0.00" can display as
*   0.00 in the detail view.
*
*   This small presentation difference is explicitly accepted because
*   custom Gateway JSON serialization solely for this field would add
*   disproportionate implementation and maintenance complexity.
*
*   Backend presence information must remain separate from the numeric
*   value. A value of 0.00 must not be used as proof that the source
*   record existed.
*
* Accepted examples:
*
*   EDGEWTDAMT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   GRVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   INVVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   INVVALUE_CAL:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
*   LASTMONTHWRKCOMP:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="0.00"
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   EDGEWTDAMT:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="1.00"
*
*   GRVALUE_USD:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="-1.00"
*
*   LASTMONTHWRKCOMP:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="10.00"
*
* The following direction is also rejected:
*
*   XSA="0.00", SEGW=null
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'EDGEWTDAMT'
      OR 'GRVALUE_USD'
      OR 'INVVALUE_CAL'
      OR 'INVVALUE_USD'
      OR 'LASTMONTHWRKCOMP'.

        rv_result =
        xsdbool(
          i_actual_token = '"0.00"'
        ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Time property serialized by SEGW as "PT00H00M00S"
*
* CHANGEDTIME
*   Initial ABAP TIMS value 000000 is also a valid midnight value.
*   Standard SEGW therefore serializes it as:
*
*     "PT00H00M00S"
*
*   XSA preserves an absent database value as:
*
*     null
*
*   The active UI primarily uses CHANGED_ON for the complete update
*   timestamp. The SEGW zero-time representation is therefore accepted
*   as a known compatibility deviation.
*
* Accepted example:
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="PT00H00M00S"
*
* Rejected examples:
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA=null, SEGW="PT10H15M00S"
*
*   CHANGEDTIME:
*     XSA="PT00H00M00S", SEGW=null
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
      WHEN 'CHANGEDTIME'.

        rv_result =
        xsdbool(
          i_actual_token = '"PT00H00M00S"'
        ).

    ENDCASE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD normalize_odata_time.

    DATA:
      lv_value       TYPE string,
      lv_remainder   TYPE string,
      lv_hour_text   TYPE string,
      lv_minute_text TYPE string,
      lv_second_text TYPE string,
      lv_hour        TYPE i,
      lv_minute      TYPE i,
      lv_second      TYPE i,
      lv_offset      TYPE i.

    rv_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_token ).
    IF strlen( rv_token ) < 2 OR rv_token(1) <> '"'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_value =
      unquote_json(
        i_token = rv_token ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* A supported value must start with PT and contain H, M, and S.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF strlen( lv_value ) < 7
       OR lv_value(2) <> 'PT'
       OR lv_value NP 'PT*H*M*S'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_value
        off = 2 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract hours.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'H'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_hour_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = lv_offset + 1 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract minutes.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'M'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_minute_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

    lv_remainder =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = lv_offset + 1 ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Extract seconds and reject any trailing content.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    FIND FIRST OCCURRENCE OF 'S'
      IN lv_remainder
      MATCH OFFSET lv_offset.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0
       OR lv_offset = 0
       OR lv_offset + 1 <> strlen( lv_remainder ).
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_second_text =
      substring(
        val = lv_remainder
        off = 0
        len = lv_offset ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Reject non-numeric components before conversion.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_hour_text CN '0123456789'
       OR lv_minute_text CN '0123456789'
       OR lv_second_text CN '0123456789'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    IF strlen( lv_hour_text ) > 2 OR strlen( lv_minute_text ) > 2
       OR strlen( lv_second_text ) > 2.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_hour =
      CONV i( lv_hour_text ).

    lv_minute =
      CONV i( lv_minute_text ).

    lv_second =
      CONV i( lv_second_text ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Validate ordinary time-of-day ranges.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lv_hour > 23
       OR lv_minute > 59
       OR lv_second > 59.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Canonical JSON token with two-digit components.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    rv_token =
        '"PT'
      && |{ lv_hour WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'H'
      && |{ lv_minute WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'M'
      && |{ lv_second WIDTH = 2 PAD = '0' ALIGN = RIGHT }|
      && 'S"'.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD ignore_missing_property.

    rv_result =
      xsdbool( i_name = 'ID' ).

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD is_unordered_aggregate_field.

    CASE i_name.

      WHEN 'GLACCOUNT'
        OR 'COSTCENTER'
        OR 'COSTCENTERDESC'
        OR 'MANAGEUNIT'
        OR 'INTERNALORDER'
        OR 'ASSETID'
        OR 'PROFITCENTER'
        OR 'BBP_ACC_NO'
        OR 'DISTPERCENTAGE'
        OR 'RULETYPE'
        OR 'RULETYPETEXT'.

        rv_result =
          abap_true.

    ENDCASE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD equal_aggregate_content.

    DATA:
      lt_expected TYPE tt_string,
      lt_actual   TYPE tt_string.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Semantic unordered parity:
* - order of all segments is ignored;
* - leading, internal and trailing empty segments are preserved;
* - number of empty segments must still match.
*
* Therefore:
*   ;A;B     = A;B;
*   ;;A;B    = A;B;;
* but:
*   A;;B     <> A;B
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lt_expected =
      split_aggregate_empty(
        i_value = i_expected ).

    lt_actual =
      split_aggregate_empty(
        i_value = i_actual ).

    IF lines( lt_expected ) <>
       lines( lt_actual ).

      FREE:
        lt_expected,
        lt_actual.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    SORT lt_expected
      BY table_line.

    SORT lt_actual
      BY table_line.

    rv_equal =
      xsdbool(
        lt_expected = lt_actual ).

    FREE:
      lt_expected,
      lt_actual.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD split_aggregate_empty.

    DATA:
      lv_length TYPE i,
      lv_start  TYPE i,
      lv_pos    TYPE i,
      lv_token  TYPE string.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Empty aggregate represents zero aggregate values, not one empty
* account-assignment slot. This follows the existing XSA convention.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF i_value IS INITIAL.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_value ).

    WHILE lv_pos < lv_length.

      IF i_value+lv_pos(1) = ';'.

        lv_token =
          substring(
            val = i_value
            off = lv_start
            len = lv_pos - lv_start ).

        APPEND lv_token
          TO rt_tokens.

        CLEAR lv_token.

        lv_start =
          lv_pos + 1.

      ENDIF.

      lv_pos =
        lv_pos + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Always append the final segment. If the source ends in ';', this is
* deliberately an empty token.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    lv_token =
      substring(
        val = i_value
        off = lv_start
        len = lv_length - lv_start ).

    APPEND lv_token
      TO rt_tokens.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_string_end.

    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_escape   TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).
    IF i_start < 0 OR i_start >= lv_length
       OR i_text+i_start(1) <> '"'.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason =
                     'JSON string does not start with quote'.

    ENDIF.

    lv_position =
      i_start + 1.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_escape = abap_true.
        IF lv_char = 'u'.
          IF lv_position + 4 >= lv_length.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'Truncated JSON Unicode escape'.
          ENDIF.
          DATA(lv_digits) = substring( val = i_text off = lv_position + 1 len = 4 ).
          IF lv_digits CN '0123456789abcdefABCDEF'.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON Unicode escape'.
          ENDIF.
          DATA lv_unicode_hex TYPE x LENGTH 2.
          lv_unicode_hex = to_upper( lv_digits ).
          DATA(lv_unicode_code) = CONV i( lv_unicode_hex ).
          IF lv_unicode_code >= 55296 AND lv_unicode_code <= 56319.
            IF lv_position + 10 >= lv_length.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = |Missing JSON low surrogate; Helper=read_json_string_end; offset={ lv_position }; length={ lv_length }|.
            ENDIF.
            IF substring( val = i_text off = lv_position + 5 len = 2 ) <> '\u'.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = |Missing JSON low surrogate; Helper=read_json_string_end; offset={ lv_position }; length={ lv_length }|.
            ENDIF.
            lv_digits = substring( val = i_text off = lv_position + 7 len = 4 ).
            IF lv_digits CN '0123456789abcdefABCDEF'.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = |Invalid JSON low surrogate; offset={ lv_position + 7 }|.
            ENDIF.
            lv_unicode_hex = to_upper( lv_digits ).
            lv_unicode_code = CONV i( lv_unicode_hex ).
            IF lv_unicode_code < 56320 OR lv_unicode_code > 57343.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = |Invalid JSON low surrogate; offset={ lv_position + 7 }|.
            ENDIF.
            lv_position = lv_position + 6.
          ELSEIF lv_unicode_code >= 56320 AND lv_unicode_code <= 57343.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = |Unpaired JSON low surrogate; offset={ lv_position }|.
          ENDIF.
          lv_position = lv_position + 4.
        ELSEIF lv_char <> '"' AND lv_char <> '\' AND lv_char <> '/'
           AND lv_char <> 'b' AND lv_char <> 'f' AND lv_char <> 'n'
           AND lv_char <> 'r' AND lv_char <> 't'.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON string escape'.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR lv_escape.

      ELSEIF lv_char = '\'.

        lv_escape =
          abap_true.

      ELSEIF lv_char = '"'.

        rv_end =
          lv_position.

        RETURN.
      ELSEIF lv_char < space.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'Unescaped JSON control character'.
      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      EXPORTING
        i_reason =
                   'JSON string is not closed'.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD read_json_value_end.

    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_end      TYPE i.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).
    IF i_start < 0 OR i_start >= lv_length.

      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason =
                     'JSON value is missing'.

    ENDIF.

    lv_char =
      i_text+i_start(1).

    IF lv_char = '"'.

      lv_end =
        read_json_string_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    IF lv_char = '{'.

      lv_end =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start
          i_open  = '{'
          i_close = '}' ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.
      DATA(lt_checked_properties) = read_object_properties(
        i_json = i_text i_object_from = i_start i_object_to = lv_end ).
      FREE lt_checked_properties.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    IF lv_char = '['.

      lv_end =
        find_matching_end(
          i_text  = i_text
          i_start = i_start
          i_open  = '['
          i_close = ']' ).

      rv_end =
        lv_end + 1.
      lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_text i_position = i_start + 1 ).
      WHILE lv_position < lv_end.
        lv_position = read_json_value_end( i_text = i_text i_start = lv_position ).
        lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_text i_position = lv_position ).
        IF lv_position = lv_end.
          EXIT.
        ENDIF.
        IF lv_position > lv_end OR i_text+lv_position(1) <> ','.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'JSON array values require a comma'.
        ENDIF.
        lv_position = skip_whitespace( i_text = i_text i_position = lv_position + 1 ).
        IF lv_position >= lv_end.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'Trailing JSON array comma'.
        ENDIF.
      ENDWHILE.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_position =
      i_start.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_char = ','
         OR lv_char = '}' OR lv_char = ']'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    DATA(lv_primitive) = trim_text( substring(
      val = i_text off = i_start len = lv_position - i_start ) ).
    FIND REGEX '^(null|true|false|-?(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]+)?([eE][+-]?[0-9]+)?)$'
      IN lv_primitive.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid or empty JSON primitive'.
    ENDIF.
    rv_end = lv_position.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD find_matching_end.

    DATA:
      lv_position TYPE i,
      lv_length   TYPE i,
      lv_depth    TYPE i,
      lv_char     TYPE char1,
      lv_in_text  TYPE abap_bool,
      lv_escape   TYPE abap_bool.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).
    IF i_start < 0 OR i_start >= lv_length.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason =
          |Helper=find_matching_end; invalid offset={ i_start }; length={ lv_length }|.
    ENDIF.
    IF i_text+i_start(1) <> i_open.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason =
          |Helper=find_matching_end; expected="{ i_open }"; offset={ i_start }; length={ lv_length }|.
    ENDIF.

    lv_position =
      i_start.

    WHILE lv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_position(1).

      IF lv_in_text = abap_true.

        IF lv_escape = abap_true.

          CLEAR lv_escape.

        ELSEIF lv_char = '\'.

          lv_escape =
            abap_true.

        ELSEIF lv_char = '"'.

          CLEAR lv_in_text.

        ENDIF.

      ELSE.

        IF lv_char = '"'.

          lv_in_text =
            abap_true.

        ELSEIF lv_char = i_open.

          lv_depth =
            lv_depth + 1.

        ELSEIF lv_char = i_close.

          lv_depth =
            lv_depth - 1.

          IF lv_depth = 0.

            rv_end =
              lv_position.

            RETURN.

          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.

      ENDIF.

      lv_position =
        lv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
      EXPORTING
        i_reason =
                   'JSON object or array is not closed'.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD skip_whitespace.

    DATA:
      lv_length TYPE i,
      lv_char   TYPE char1.

    rv_position =
      i_position.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).
    IF i_position < 0 OR i_position > lv_length.
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason =
          |Helper=skip_whitespace; invalid offset={ i_position }; length={ lv_length }|.
    ENDIF.

    WHILE rv_position < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+rv_position(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        RETURN.

      ENDIF.

      rv_position =
        rv_position + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD is_whitespace.

    IF i_char = space
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+0(1)
       OR i_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+1(1).

      rv_result =
        abap_true.

    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD trim_text.

    DATA:
      lv_start         TYPE i,
      lv_end           TYPE i,
      lv_length        TYPE i,
      lv_last_position TYPE i,
      lv_char          TYPE char1.

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        i_text ).

    lv_end =
      lv_length.

    WHILE lv_start < lv_length.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_start(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        EXIT.

      ENDIF.

      lv_start =
        lv_start + 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    WHILE lv_end > lv_start.

      lv_last_position =
        lv_end - 1.

      lv_char =
        i_text+lv_last_position(1).

      IF is_whitespace(
           i_char = lv_char ) = abap_false.

        EXIT.

      ENDIF.

      lv_end =
        lv_end - 1.

    ENDWHILE.

    IF lv_end > lv_start.

      rv_text =
        substring(
          val = i_text
          off = lv_start
          len = lv_end - lv_start ).

    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD unquote_json.

    DATA:
      lv_token        TYPE string,
      lv_length       TYPE i,
      lv_last_offset  TYPE i,
      lv_inner_length TYPE i,
      lv_first_char   TYPE char1,
      lv_last_char    TYPE char1.

    lv_token =
      trim_text(
        i_text = i_token ).

    lv_length =
      strlen(
        lv_token ).

    IF lv_length < 2.

      rv_value =
        lv_token.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_last_offset =
      lv_length - 1.

    lv_first_char =
      lv_token(1).

    lv_last_char =
      lv_token+lv_last_offset(1).

    IF lv_first_char <> '"'
       OR lv_last_char <> '"'.

      rv_value =
        lv_token.

      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_inner_length =
      lv_length - 2.

    rv_value =
      substring(
        val = lv_token
        off = 1
        len = lv_inner_length ).

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Avoid replacement scans in the normal case where the JSON value has
* no escaped characters.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF rv_value NS '\'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    DATA(lv_source) = rv_value.
    CLEAR rv_value.
    DATA lv_position TYPE i.
    DATA lv_hex TYPE x LENGTH 2.
    DATA lv_codepoint TYPE i.
    DATA lv_units TYPE i.
    DATA lv_utf16 TYPE xstring.
    DATA lv_unicode TYPE c LENGTH 2.
    TRY.
    WHILE lv_position < lv_inner_length.
      DATA(lv_char) = substring( val = lv_source off = lv_position len = 1 ).
      lv_position = lv_position + 1.
      IF lv_char <> '\'.
        rv_value = rv_value && lv_char.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
      IF lv_position >= lv_inner_length.
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
          EXPORTING i_reason = 'Truncated JSON escape'.
      ENDIF.
      DATA(lv_escape) = substring( val = lv_source off = lv_position len = 1 ).
      lv_position = lv_position + 1.
      CASE lv_escape.
        WHEN '"' OR '\' OR '/'.
          lv_char = lv_escape.
        WHEN 'n'.
          lv_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline.
        WHEN 'r'.
          lv_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>cr_lf+0(1).
        WHEN 't'.
          lv_char = cl_abap_char_utilities=>horizontal_tab.
        WHEN 'b' OR 'f'.
          lv_codepoint = COND #( WHEN lv_escape = 'b' THEN 8 ELSE 12 ).
          lv_unicode = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>uccpi( lv_codepoint ).
          CONCATENATE lv_unicode(1) `` INTO lv_char RESPECTING BLANKS.
        WHEN 'u'.
          IF lv_position + 4 > lv_inner_length.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'Truncated JSON Unicode escape'.
          ENDIF.
          DATA(lv_digits) = substring( val = lv_source off = lv_position len = 4 ).
          IF lv_digits CN '0123456789abcdefABCDEF'.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON Unicode escape'.
          ENDIF.
          lv_hex = to_upper( lv_digits ).
          lv_codepoint = CONV i( lv_hex ).
          lv_position = lv_position + 4.
          lv_units = 1.
          IF lv_codepoint >= 55296 AND lv_codepoint <= 56319.
            DATA(lv_high_surrogate) = lv_hex.
            IF lv_position + 6 > lv_inner_length.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = 'Missing JSON low surrogate'.
            ENDIF.
            IF substring( val = lv_source off = lv_position len = 2 ) <> '\u'.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = 'Missing JSON low surrogate'.
            ENDIF.
            lv_digits = substring( val = lv_source off = lv_position + 2 len = 4 ).
            IF lv_digits CN '0123456789abcdefABCDEF'.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON low surrogate'.
            ENDIF.
            lv_hex = to_upper( lv_digits ).
            DATA(lv_low_surrogate) = CONV i( lv_hex ).
            IF lv_low_surrogate < 56320 OR lv_low_surrogate > 57343.
              RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
                EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON low surrogate'.
            ENDIF.
            CONCATENATE lv_high_surrogate lv_hex INTO lv_utf16 IN BYTE MODE.
            lv_position = lv_position + 6.
            lv_units = 2.
          ELSEIF lv_codepoint >= 56320 AND lv_codepoint <= 57343.
            RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
              EXPORTING i_reason = 'Unpaired JSON low surrogate'.
          ENDIF.
          IF lv_units = 2.
            DATA(lo_utf16) = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>create(
              encoding = '4102' input = lv_utf16 ).
            lo_utf16->read( IMPORTING data = lv_char ).
          ELSE.
            lv_unicode = cl_abap_conv_in_ce=>uccpi( lv_codepoint ).
            CONCATENATE lv_unicode(1) `` INTO lv_char RESPECTING BLANKS.
          ENDIF.
        WHEN OTHERS.
          RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE zcx_fi_das_error
            EXPORTING i_reason = 'Invalid JSON escape'.
      ENDCASE.
      rv_value = rv_value && lv_char.
    ENDWHILE.
    CATCH zcx_fi_das_error cx_sy_conversion_error cx_sy_range_out_of_bounds
          INTO DATA(lx_decode).
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING
          i_reason = |Helper=unquote_json; offset={ lv_position }; |
                  && |token length={ lv_length }; inner length={ lv_inner_length }; |
                  && exception_text( lx_decode )
          previous = lx_decode.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD add_difference.

    mv_difference_count =
      mv_difference_count + 1.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Count every mismatch, but retain a bounded diagnostic sample. This
* prevents failures with many missing rows from allocating huge logs.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    IF lines( mt_differences ) >= mc_max_log.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.

    APPEND bounded_text( i_text = i_text i_limit = 2048 )
      TO mt_differences.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD difference_text.

    DATA:
      lv_text   TYPE string,
      lv_line   TYPE string,
      lv_shown  TYPE i,
      lv_hidden TYPE i.

    IF mv_difference_count = 0.
      RETURN.

    ENDIF.

    lv_shown =
      lines(
        mt_differences ).

    lv_hidden =
      mv_difference_count
      - lv_shown.

    lv_text =
        |{ i_title } |
      && |Difference count: { mv_difference_count }.|
      && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
      && |--- First { lv_shown } difference(s) ---|.

    LOOP AT mt_differences INTO lv_line.

      lv_text =
          lv_text
        && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
        && lv_line.

    ENDLOOP.

    IF lv_hidden > 0.

      lv_text =
          lv_text
        && cl_abap_char_utilities=>newline
        && |--- { lv_hidden } further difference(s) omitted ---|.

    ENDIF.

    rv_text = lv_text.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD assert_no_differences.
    IF mv_difference_count > 0.
      mv_parity_reported = abap_true.
      report_failure( difference_text( i_title ) ).
    ENDIF.

  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD extract_range.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #( filter_select_options = it_filters ) ).
    mv_phase = 'Range extraction / extract_range -> extract_filters -> convert_range_to_where'.
    CLEAR mv_request.
    LOOP AT it_filters INTO DATA(ls_input).
      IF sy-tabix > 4.
        mv_request = mv_request && '; further input ranges omitted'.
        EXIT.
      ENDIF.
      mv_request = mv_request && |[{ ls_input-property } { ls_input-sign }/{ ls_input-option }; |
        && |low="{ bounded_text( i_text = ls_input-low i_limit = 64 ) }" (length={ strlen( ls_input-low ) }); |
        && |high="{ bounded_text( i_text = ls_input-high i_limit = 64 ) }" (length={ strlen( ls_input-high ) })]|.
    ENDLOOP.
    TRY.
        mo_cut->extract_filters( CHANGING cs_request = ls_request ).
      CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_ranges).
        RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
          EXPORTING
            i_reason = |input ranges={ lines( it_filters ) }; |
                    && |remaining={ lines( ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ) }; |
                    && exception_text( lx_ranges )
            previous = lx_ranges.
    ENDTRY.
    DATA(lv_remaining_ranges) = lines( ls_request-filter-filter_select_options ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_remaining_ranges
      exp  = 0
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (extract_range): Unconsumed input filter ranges, not result rows; |
          && |input={ lines( it_filters ) }, remaining={ lv_remaining_ranges }, |
          && |SQL="{ bounded_text( ls_request-filter-filter_string ) }"; |
          && |PO SQL="{ bounded_text( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-ponumber ) }"; |
          && |ITEM SQL="{ bounded_text( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-item ) }"; |
          && |SUPPLIER SQL="{ bounded_text( ls_request-filter_for_amdp-suppliername ) }"; |
          && |Request={ mv_request }; |
          && |Activate dashboard c2bf1b3; ranges must be copied/cleared before SHDB conversion| ).
    rs_request = ls_request.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD filter_rows.
    DATA(ls_request) = VALUE zcl_fi_das_dashboard=>ts_internal_request(
      filter = VALUE #( filter_string = i_predicate ) ).
    mv_phase = 'HANA row filtering / filter_rows -> apply_generic_filter'.
    mv_request = |SQL="{ bounded_text( i_predicate ) }"; input rows={ lines( ct_result ) }|.
    TRY.
    mo_cut->apply_generic_filter(
      EXPORTING is_request = ls_request CHANGING ct_result = ct_result ).
    CATCH zcx_fi_das_error INTO DATA(lx_filter).
      RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE lcx_parity_error
        EXPORTING i_reason = exception_text( lx_filter ) previous = lx_filter.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD check_select.
    DATA(ls_case) = VALUE ts_case(
      email = 'adam.strasky@takeda.com'
      flow = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_over
      pstype = zif_fi_das_dashboard=>mc_pstype_service
      select_list = i_select orderby = i_orderby
      top = 1 top_specified = abap_true ).
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
                          IMPORTING e_json  = DATA(lv_selected) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
      act  = get_result_count( lv_selected )
      exp  = 1 ).
    DATA(lv_po) = read_first_result_property(
      i_json     = lv_selected
      i_property = 'PONUMBER' ).
    DATA(lv_item) = read_first_result_property(
      i_json     = lv_selected
      i_property = 'ITEMNO' ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
      act  = lv_po ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
      act  = lv_item ).
    CLEAR: ls_case-select_list, ls_case-orderby.
    ls_case-filter_raw = |PONUMBER eq '{ odata_quote( lv_po ) }' and |
                     && |ITEMNO eq '{ odata_quote( lv_item ) }'|.
    get_gateway_response( EXPORTING is_case = ls_case
                          IMPORTING e_json  = DATA(lv_full) ).
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
      act  = get_result_count( lv_full )
      exp  = 1 ).
    find_result_object_bounds(
      EXPORTING i_json = lv_selected
                i_index = 1
      IMPORTING e_object_from = DATA(lv_from) e_object_to = DATA(lv_to) ).
    DATA(lt_selected) = read_object_properties(
      i_json        = lv_selected
      i_object_from = lv_from
      i_object_to   = lv_to ).
    DATA lt_fields TYPE stringtab.
    SPLIT i_select AT ',' INTO TABLE lt_fields.
    LOOP AT lt_fields INTO DATA(lv_field).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = xsdbool( line_exists( lt_selected[ name = lv_field ] ) )
        msg  = |Restricted select omitted { lv_field }| ).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act  = read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_selected i_property = lv_field )
        exp  = read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_full i_property = lv_field )
        msg  = |Restricted select must retain { lv_field } for the same PO/item| ).
      IF lv_field = 'STATUS'.
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_not_initial(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
          act  =
          read_first_result_property( i_json = lv_selected i_property = lv_field ) ).
        cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_false(
          quit = mv_assert_quit
          msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (check_select)|
          act  = xsdbool( lt_selected[ name = lv_field ]-token = 'null' ) ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    LOOP AT lt_selected INTO DATA(ls_selected).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        act = xsdbool( ls_selected-name = 'PONUMBER'
                       OR ls_selected-name = 'ITEMNO'
                       OR line_exists( lt_fields[ table_line = ls_selected-name ] ) )
        msg = |Restricted select returned nonselected property { ls_selected-name }| ).
    ENDLOOP.
    assert_inlinecount_absent( lv_selected ).
  ENDMETHOD.

  METHOD matches_complex_fixture.
    DATA lt_names TYPE stringtab.
    DATA: lv_po TYPE string, lv_item TYPE string, lv_desc TYPE string,
          lv_supplier TYPE string, lv_date TYPE string.
    lt_names = VALUE #( ( `PONUMBER` ) ( `ITEMNO` ) ( `POLINEDESC` )
                       ( `POCREATEDAT` ) ( `SUPPLIERNAME` ) ).
    LOOP AT lt_names INTO DATA(lv_name).
      cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_true(
        quit = mv_assert_quit
        msg  = |{ mv_group_test } (matches_complex_fixture)|
        act  = xsdbool( line_exists( it_properties[ name = lv_name ] ) ) ).
      IF NOT line_exists( it_properties[ name = lv_name ] ).
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      IF it_properties[ name = lv_name ]-token = 'null'.
        RETURN.
      ENDIF.
      DATA(lv_token) = it_properties[ name = lv_name ]-token.
      DATA(lv_value) = unquote_json( lv_token ).
      CASE lv_name.
        WHEN 'PONUMBER'. lv_po = lv_value.
        WHEN 'ITEMNO'. lv_item = lv_value.
        WHEN 'POLINEDESC'. lv_desc = lv_value.
        WHEN 'POCREATEDAT'. lv_date = lv_value.
        WHEN 'SUPPLIERNAME'. lv_supplier = lv_value.
      ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
    DATA lv_milliseconds TYPE string.
    FIND REGEX '^/Date\(([0-9]+)\)/$' IN lv_date SUBMATCHES lv_milliseconds.
    DATA(lv_date_subrc) = sy-subrc.
    cl_abap_unit_assert=>assert_equals(
      quit = mv_assert_quit
      act  = lv_date_subrc
      exp  = 0
      msg  = 'The XSA date oracle requires an epoch-millisecond token' ).
    IF lv_date_subrc <> 0.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
    " The PO operands remain strings; NUMC/numeric comparison changes this expression.
    rv_match = xsdbool(
      find( val  = lv_desc
            sub  = 'b'
            case = abap_true ) < 0
      AND find( val  = lv_po
                sub  = '5'
                case = abap_true ) < 0
      AND find( val  = lv_desc
                sub  = 'a'
                case = abap_true ) >= 0
      AND CONV int8( lv_milliseconds ) > CONV int8( '1760400000000' )
      AND ( find( val  = lv_supplier
                  sub  = 'B'
                  case = abap_true ) = 0
         OR find( val  = lv_supplier
                  sub  = 'A'
                  case = abap_true ) >= 0 )
      AND ( lv_po <= `977` OR lv_po > `9000600000`
         OR ( lv_po >= `8000006000` AND lv_po <= `8000906000` )
         OR lv_po = `8000096102` OR lv_po = `8000097224` )
      AND ( lv_item = '00001' OR lv_item = '00003' OR lv_item = '00004' ) ).
  ENDMETHOD.
ENDCLASS.
